Method: EvoKnow 采用了一种新颖的方法，通过保留从基础领域学习的共享先验知识，增量地添加隔离的残差专家来补充生成器相关的证据，并通过从当前生成图像和累积的充分统计量更新的 Analytical Incremental Router (AIR) 来检索专业知识。

Result: 实验显示，EvoKnow 能够实现有效的跨生成器泛化、少量样本扩展以及长期的持续适应。即使每种新生成器只有十个生成图像，EvoKnow 也能在没有目标基准调整的情况下达到96.70%的平均准确性，且在严格无重播持续学习协议下，其效率达到当前最先进的持续学习性能。

Conclusion: EvoKnow 为持续的 AI 生成图像检测提供了一种创新的方法，能够有效应对不断变化的生成器模型，并在多个评测指标上展现出色表现。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors are commonly trained on fixed generator domains and become difficult to maintain as new generative models emerge. Continual adaptation is challenging because replaying historical generated images is costly, whereas updating shared parameters with limited current-domain data can overwrite prior forensic knowledge. We propose EvoKnow, a replay-free framework that formulates continual AI-generated image detection as forensic knowledge evolution. EvoKnow preserves a shared forensic basis learned from base domains, incrementally adds isolated residual experts for complementary generator-relevant evidence, and retrieves expertise through an Analytical Incremental Router (AIR) updated in closed form from current-stage generated images and accumulated sufficient statistics. Experiments demonstrate effective cross-generator generalization, few-shot expansion, and long-horizon continual adaptation. With ten generated images per arriving generator, EvoKnow achieves 96.70% average accuracy on non-base GenImage generators and 94.48% accuracy on Chameleon without target-benchmark adaptation. Under a strict replay-free continual learning protocol, EvoKnow achieves state-of-the-art continual learning performance, attaining 96.32% mean stage-wise accuracy and 4.32% average forgetting.

</details>


### [73] [GroundSight at GroundLM 2026 Shared Tasks: GoldenViewVQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11402)
*Kun Wang,Yupeng Hu,Ruping Cao,Hao Liu,Zhiran Li,Qianlong Xiang,Harry Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoVeR-VQA 提出了一种无需训练的多阶段验证和修正框架，用于基于多视图的 VQA 任务，通过逐步应用特定视图验证、先验指导联合验证和跨划分组级验证，显著提升了金字段 VQA 测试集上的综合准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VQA 方法主要关注答案准确性，而 CoVeR-VQA 强调精确的证据定位同样重要，特别是在驾驶场景中需确认支持视觉证据的情况，以确保结论可靠性。

Method: CoVeR-VQA 采用 GPT-5.6 的零样本预测作为起点，通过三次迭代的特定视图验证、先验指导联合验证和跨划分组级验证，逐步提升模型性能。

Result: 在官方金字段 VQA 测试集上，CoVeR-VQA 的四阶段框架达到了 84.75% 的综合准确率，比 GPT-5.6 的零样本基准提高了 13.56 个百分点，答案准确率为 94.92%，视图准确率为 86.44%。最终提交的版本在两个额外的人工后处理修正后，综合准确率达到 88.14%。

Conclusion: CoVeR-VQA 突出表明支持视图的定位是剩余错误的主要来源，进一步强调了明确的证据验证对可靠多视图多模态推理的重要性。

Abstract: GoldenViewVQA requires models to jointly answer driving-scene questions and identify the camera view containing the supporting visual evidence, making precise evidence localization as important as answer correctness. We present \textbf{CoVeR-VQA}, a training-free multi-stage verification and correction framework for grounded multi-view VQA. Starting from GPT-5.6 zero-shot predictions, CoVeR-VQA progressively applies view-specific verification with Gemini-3.6-Flash, prior-guided joint verification with Claude-Opus-5, and cross-split group-level verification that exploits semantically filtered question groups from shared multi-view scenes and validation-derived prior knowledge. On the official GoldenViewVQA test set, the four-stage CoVeR-VQA pipeline achieves 84.75\% Joint Accuracy, improving the GPT-5.6 zero-shot baseline by 13.56 percentage points, while reaching 94.92\% Answer Accuracy and 86.44\% View Accuracy. The final submitted run achieves 88.14\% Joint Accuracy after two additional evaluator-informed post-hoc corrections. Our analysis shows that supporting-view localization remains the primary source of residual errors, highlighting the importance of explicit evidence verification for reliable multi-view multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [74] [Fresco++: Frequency-Guided and Canonical-Consistent Optimization for Fine-Grained Head Avatar Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11412)
*Shikun Zhang,Yong Li,Yiqun Wang,Qiuhong Ke,Cunjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fresco++是一个统一的优化框架，旨在实现细粒度且视图一致的头像重建。通过频率意识和跨视图优化策略，优化过程从低频结构开始，逐步引入高频细节约束，同时保持跨视图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头像优化通常依赖于单一视角的图像监督，这可能导致初学者过度拟合不稳定高频细节，以及不同视角下局部外观不一致。Fresco++通过分阶段稳定低频结构并引入高频细节约束来解决这些挑战，并建立一种一致性观察方法。

Method: Fresco++结合了频率意识和跨视图优化策略。首先采用渐进课程法，先稳定低频结构，再引入高频细节约束；其次引入Canonical Group Consensus，通过共享标准基准表面区域关联局部观察，并在特征空间中综合多视角证据形成共识目标，从而在不影响特定图像空间参数化的情况下强制局部一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，Fresco++在NeRSemble上的头像重建质量得到提升，跨视角一致性也得到了增强，且在不同avatar表示上也展示出适用性和转移性。

Conclusion: Fresco++提供了一种稳定从粗到细的优化过程，同时保持视图间的一致性，解决了头像优化中的关键挑战。

Abstract: We propose Fresco++, a unified optimization framework for fine-grained and view-consistent head avatar reconstruction. Head avatar optimization is typically driven by per-view image supervision, which can lead to premature fitting of unstable high-frequency details and inconsistent local appearance across viewpoints. Fresco++ addresses these challenges by regulating both the progression of visual detail and the formation of cross-view supervision during optimization. For frequency-aware optimization, a progressive curriculum first stabilizes low-frequency structures and then introduces high-frequency constraints to recover fine facial and hair details without amplifying spurious responses at early stages. For cross-view optimization, we introduce Canonical Group Consensus, which associates local observations through shared canonical surface regions and establishes correspondence across different viewpoints. Geometric and visibility-aware screening removes unreliable observations, while the remaining multi-view evidence is aggregated in feature space to form a consensus target for supervising the current rendering. This design enforces local consistency without relying on a specific image-space parameterization and avoids additional rendering of the auxiliary view. Together, the frequency curriculum and canonical consensus provide stable optimization from coarse structures to fine details while maintaining coherent appearance across viewpoints. Extensive experiments on NeRSemble demonstrate improved reconstruction quality and cross-view consistency, while evaluations across diverse avatar representations further confirm the generality and transferability of Fresco++.

</details>


### [75] [Missing Modality-Aware Calibration for Trustworthy Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11419)
*Sol Lee,Hyunji Kim,Sungrae Hong,Donghee Han,Mun Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种针对模态缺失的局部温度调整方法（MMA-LTS），旨在提高脑肿瘤分割模型在不同缺失模态场景下的置信度校准性能，同时保持分割准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脑肿瘤分割方法在模态缺失条件下虽然能够保持分割准确性，但通常忽视了预测可靠性，导致临床应用中的校准不准确。

Method: MMA-LTS 是一种后处理的体素级置信度校准方法，通过模态可用性可学习标记和体素级难度评分估计一个空间自适应温度场。

Result: 实验结果表明，MMA-LTS 在 BraTS 2020 和 FeTS 2024 数据集上的表现优于或接近当前最先进的方法，且在多种模态缺失场景下均能提升校准性能。

Conclusion: 该研究的 MMA-LTS 方法为脑肿瘤分割在临床实际应用中提供了更可靠和可信的模型置信度校准方案。

Abstract: Multimodal brain tumor segmentation typically leverages multiple MRI modalities, yet incomplete modality acquisition is common in clinical practice due to protocol heterogeneity and scan failures. Although recent methods maintain segmentation accuracy under missing modality conditions, they frequently overlook prediction reliability, leading to miscalibrated confidence estimates that hinder clinical adoption. Existing calibration techniques are largely modality-agnostic or assume that prediction difficulty decreases monotonically as additional modalities become available. However, in brain tumor segmentation, prediction difficulty depends primarily on which modalities are absent rather than how many, leading to combination-specific and spatially heterogeneous calibration errors. To address this, we propose Missing Modality-Aware Local Temperature Scaling (MMA-LTS), a post-hoc voxel-wise confidence calibration method. It estimates a spatially adaptive temperature field conditioned on a modality-availability learnable token and a voxel-wise difficulty score. Experiments on BraTS 2020 and FeTS 2024 show that MMA-LTS improves calibration while preserving the segmentation accuracy of state-of-the-art models across diverse missing-modality scenarios, thereby enhancing trustworthiness toward clinical deployment.

</details>


### [76] [EchoDiST: Self-distillation-based joint learning for diffusion-conditioned echocardiographic myocardial motion estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11431)
*Feiyue Qi,Xingyue Wei,Jianwen Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了EchoDiST框架，一种基于自蒸馏的联合学习与扩散条件化的无监督心脏超声心肌运动估计方法，显著改善了心脏结构对齐、心肌应变评估和基于运动的功能及心动周期评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文章指出心脏超声心动图中的心肌运动估计是定量评估心脏功能和心肌力学的关键，但由于图像中的伪影、有限的信息、斑点相关性和缺乏准确的位移场参考，该过程依然充满挑战。

Method: 设计了一个名为EchoDiST的框架，结合非监督学习、自蒸馏策略和基于扩散的条件化方法来估计心肌运动。该方法能有效利用有限的解剖注释信息进行心肌运动估计，并在训练过程中引入随机扰动。推断时仅需一次确定性前向传递。

Result: 在三项心脏超声数据集上的评估结果表明，相比七种主流的学习方法，EchoDiST在心脏结构对齐、心肌应变评估和基于运动的功能及心动周期评估方面均取得了显著提升，统计学上也有显著性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的EchoDiST框架提供了一种在有限解剖监督下可靠的心肌运动估计方法，并支持心脏功能的定量评估。

Abstract: Motion estimation in echocardiography is essential for quantitative assessment of cardiac function and myocardial mechanics, but remains challenging due to image artifacts, limited image information, speckle decorrelation, and the scarcity of ground-truth displacement fields. Anatomy-guided approaches can provide structural information, yet often rely on expert-labeled myocardial segmentations. We propose EchoDiST, a framework for unsupervised echocardiographic myocardial motion estimation that integrates self-distillation-based joint learning with a diffusion-conditioned motion estimation network. Here, unsupervised motion estimation refers to learning without ground-truth displacement fields. The self-distillation strategy jointly optimizes anatomical segmentation and myocardial motion estimation under limited anatomical annotations. Diffusion-based conditioning is used during training with stochastic perturbations, while inference requires only a single deterministic forward pass without iterative reverse-diffusion sampling. EchoDiST was evaluated on three echocardiographic datasets, including two external test datasets under cross-view and cross-dataset settings. Compared with seven representative learning-based methods, EchoDiST consistently improved anatomical alignment, myocardial strain assessment, and motion-derived functional and cardiac-phase assessment. These gains were statistically significant across the evaluated tasks and datasets. Overall, EchoDiST provides an effective approach for reliable myocardial motion estimation under limited anatomical supervision and supports downstream quantitative assessment of cardiac function.

</details>


### [77] [DLC: A Metric-Guided Dynamic Loss Controller for Multi-Objective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11433)
*Jaewan Ko,Janghoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DLC的元损失控制器，它能在训练过程中动态重新加权现有的损失项，而无需修改模型架构或引入新损失，从而在低光照图像增强、去雨和实际场景超分辨率等多个图像复原领域中优化模型的综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图像恢复方法通常使用固定权重组合的多个损失，但无法在优化过程中动态调整保真度、感知相似性和无参照质量的重要性，这限制了模型在不同任务上的效果。

Method: DLC是一种架构和损失项无关的元控制器，在训练过程中，它使用LLM（大型语言模型）根据固定的反馈子集评估当前模型的质量，并更新损失权值向量。

Result: 在低光照图像增强、去雨和实际场景超分辨率等三个领域中，DLC通过考虑不同评价指标下的权衡关系，指导训练趋向保真度和感知质量的平衡操作点，使模型在不同恢复领域中获得更好的性能。

Conclusion: DLC作为一种插件式控制器在多项图像恢复任务中表现出色，能够提升模型在多目标优化中的整体效果，具有实际应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a metric-guided dynamic loss controller (DLC) for multi-objective image restoration. Conventional image restoration pipelines usually train with a fixed weighted combination of multiple losses, without changing the relative importance of fidelity, perceptual similarity, and no-reference quality during optimization. DLC is an architecture- and loss-term-agnostic training-time controller: it does not modify the restoration architecture or introduce new differentiable loss terms, but dynamically reweights the existing training losses. During training, DLC periodically evaluates the current model on a small fixed feedback subset and uses the resulting quality metrics to update the loss-weight vector through an LLM-based controller. Because DLC operates on existing loss terms rather than task-specific architectures, the same controller formulation can be instantiated across diverse image restoration training pipelines. We evaluate DLC on three restoration domains: low-light image enhancement, deraining, and real-world super-resolution, using both reference-based and no-reference quality metrics. Across these settings, DLC considers metric-dependent trade-offs during optimization and guides training toward balanced operating points across fidelity and perceptual quality. The results show that DLC can move models toward more favorable operating points across different restoration domains, supporting its role as a practical plug-in controller for multi-objective image restoration.

</details>


### [78] [Learning to Retrieve: Internalizing Memory Retrieval for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11444)
*JiaKui Hu,Tailai Chen,Yuqi Pan,Xuerui Qiu,Jialun Liu,Xiao Cao,Zhenxin Zhu,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Bing Wang,Yanye Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: L2R 是一种新的方法，通过将记忆检索内置于生成过程中，改进了基于摄像机轨迹生成一致的 3D 场景视频，相比以往依赖外部记忆系统的方法，L2R 能够让模型更自发地学习何时和检索哪些历史信息，从而提高长期场景的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于外部记忆系统，这些系统在模型生成过程中外部运行，阻止模型自发地学习何时以及应检索哪些历史信息。L2R 的提出旨在克服这一问题。

Method: L2R 方法通过将模型的持久内部状态重新用作历史上下文的记忆，并引入检索门来选择性地访问相关的过往信息，以及引入触发器来确定何时进行检索。触发器还受到 3D 再出现信号的监督，该信号会在视野中重复出现之前观察到的内容时激活检索。

Result: 在多种基本模型和摄像机回访基准测试中，L2R 提高了长期场景的一致性，且显著减少了对外部记忆库或 3D 条件的需求。

Conclusion: L2R 是一种创新的方法，通过内化记忆检索，使得视频世界模型能够在不依赖外部记忆系统的前提下，更有效地生成一致的场景视频。

Abstract: Video world models aim to generate explorable, 3D-consistent scene videos conditioned on camera trajectories. Existing approaches often rely on external memory systems that explicitly retrieve previously observed content to mitigate scene drift during long-horizon generation. However, these auxiliary memory pathways operate outside the model's internal generative dynamics, preventing the model from intrinsically learning when and what historical information should be retrieved. We propose to internalize memory retrieval into the generation process, allowing retrieval to emerge as an intrinsic behavior of the video world model rather than relying on an external memory system. Based on this principle, we introduce \textbf{Learning-to-Retrieve (L2R)}, which repurposes the model's persistent internal state as a memory for historical context. A camera-conditioned retrieval gate selectively accesses relevant historical information from this state, determining \textit{what to retrieve}, while a retrieval trigger determines \textit{when to retrieve}. We further supervise the trigger with a 3D re-visibility signal, activating retrieval when previously observed content re-enters the current view while otherwise preserving the existing context. Together, these components enable the model to intrinsically acquire memory retrieval behavior and incorporate relevant historical observations into generation without a separate retrieval pathway. Across multiple base models and camera-revisit benchmarks, L2R improves long-term scene consistency while eliminating the need for an external memory bank or 3D conditions. https://jkhu29.github.io/l2r

</details>


### [79] [ProtoSemImage: Image-Valued Prototypes with Deformable Row Alignment for Interpretable Document Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11460)
*Mohammad Zare,Pirooz Shamsinejadbabaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种将原型表示为多通道图像的方法，以增加模型的可解释性。通过使用四通道HSV空间中的原型图像，并结合Skip-Gram目标和动态时间扭曲，模型能够更好地捕捉文本和图像之间的相似性，并在多项任务上展示了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原型分类模型中的原型通常是不可读的向量，难以解释。本文探讨了将原型表示为图像的可能性，以增强模型的可解释性，便于用户理解和分析。

Method: 该方法通过将文本中的每个标记映射为图像中的像素，使用四通道HSV空间中的原型图像来表示每个类别；并通过Skip-Gram目标学习四维瓶颈中的颜色空间，实现场到端的训练；同时采用变形行对齐进行分类，类似动态时间扭曲。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法在三项配对种子上超越了具有向量原型的其他模型，尤其是在十类任务上的性能提高了4.3到11.8个点。将图像表示固定而仅交换分类器时，可以恢复序列基线，指出匹配上的20.6个点差距而不是颜色压缩。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法证明了图像原型在分类任务中的有效性，不仅提高了性能，还增强了模型可解释性。结果显示，这种新的表示方式可以在多个任务中提供更好的分类准确性，并且在保留文本布局方面表现良好。

Abstract: Prototypes in classification models are almost always vectors, and a vector has no readable form. This paper asks what happens when a prototype is an image. Documents give the question a natural form, because a document can be rendered as a multi-channel image in which every token becomes a pixel, so a class representative can take the same shape and the same channel semantics as the inputs it stands for. ProtoSemImage represents each class by one or more visual archetypes: prototype images in a four-channel HSV space whose channels carry named linguistic factors. A Skip-Gram objective learns that color space end to end through a four-dimensional bottleneck, discourse boundary rows become differentiable typed difference rows, and classification reduces to 2D visual template matching: a deformable row alignment between a document image and the archetype bank, in the spirit of dynamic time warping. Because the match is a spatial pattern comparison rather than a linear readout, the model reports where an input departs from its archetype and along which channel, and a generative head decodes each archetype back into text. The image representation works: it beats an otherwise identical model with vector prototypes in all three paired seeds, by between 4.3 and 11.8 points on a ten-class task. The distance-based matching does not. A diagnostic that keeps the representation fixed and swaps only the classifier recovers the sequence baselines, which locates a 20.6-point shortfall in the matching rather than in the color compression, and a benchmark built so that a pair of documents shares a bag of words and differs only in arrangement confirms the layout-preservation it was designed for. We report both directions, because for a representation whose whole purpose is inspect ability, the failure modes are as informative as the gains.

</details>


### [80] [SAGE: Sink-Aware Guided Emphasis for Visual Grounding in Vision-Language Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11469)
*Jeonghyo Song,YoungJoon Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析视觉注意力'陷阱'的分层行为，并提出了一种名为SAGE的方法，旨在减少视觉证据的抑制和幻觉，从而提高了视觉定位和下游视觉-语言基准的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉-语言模型（VLMs）虽然在多种图像-文本任务上表现出色，但解码器注意力路径的缺陷会抑制视觉证据并加剧幻觉，因此需要对注意力陷阱进行更深入的研究。

Method: 研究者首先揭示了不同层的解码器展现出不同的行为模式，早期和晚期解码器层表现出提示不变的注意力崩溃，中期层则变得提示条件并推动视觉-语言对齐。基于这一洞察，他们提出了SAGE（Sink-Aware Guided Emphasis），利用与标准视觉基础模型如CLIP、ViT和DINOv3对齐的感兴趣区域（ROI）掩码来引导解码器注意力。

Result: SAGE在多种视觉编码器+解码器仅语言模型VLM家族中展现了改进的视觉定位效果，减少了幻觉，并在公共下游视觉-语言基准测试中取得一致的提升，特别是在需要局部证据的精细视觉区分场景中。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法SAGE能够有效缓解视觉-语言模型中的注意力陷阱问题，并显著提高了模型的表现。

Abstract: Recent large vision-language models (VLMs) pair a visual encoder with a large language model (LLM) and perform well on diverse image-text tasks, yet their reliability is often limited by decoder attention pathologies that suppress visual evidence and exacerbate hallucinations. In this paper, we revisit visual attention sinks and uncover a structured, layer-dependent behavior: across prompts, early and late decoder layers exhibit prompt-invariant attention collapse onto the same few image regions, which we term PIS (Prompt-Invariant Sinks), whereas mid layers become prompt-conditioned and drive vision-language alignment. This split suggests that treating sinks as a uniform effect is incomplete. Building on this insight, we propose SAGE (Sink-Aware Guided Emphasis), a lightweight intervention that steers decoder attention away from PIS and toward query-dependent regions of interest (ROIs) using token-aligned ROI masks derived from standard vision backbones such as CLIP, ViT, and DINOv3. Evaluated on diverse vision-encoder + decoder-only LLM VLM families, SAGE improves visual grounding, reduces hallucinations, and yields consistent gains across public downstream vision-language benchmarks, including fine-grained visual discrimination settings where localized evidence is crucial, when instantiated with backbone-derived ROI masks.

</details>


### [81] [Conditional Residual Prediction: Improving Autoregressive Video Diffusion without a Bidirectional Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11479)
*Bowen Zheng,Zhiguang Liu,Jiarong Ou,Rui Chen,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Conditional Residual Prediction (CRP)的方法，通过减少模型对条件的依赖，使得自因模型在生成视频时更加依赖当前输入而不是历史信息，从而提高了自因模型的生成质量。实验结果显示，使用CRP后的自因模型与双向模型之间的生成质量差距显著缩小，并且提出了一个2亿参数的自因视频生成模型Optica，其在VBench上的得分为82.78。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自因模型虽然适用于流式、交互式以及长视频生成，但在标准训练下生成质量往往低于同等规模的双向模型。本文旨在探索不依赖大型双向模型初始化或复杂的蒸馏管道的情况下，能否提升自因模型的生成质量。

Method: 提出了Conditional Residual Prediction (CRP)的方法。CRP方法使模型先预测目标而不考虑条件，条件仅作为预测的补充。将CRP应用到历史数据中，使模型尽可能从当前输入预测每一帧，仅用过去的部分信息补充缺失部分。

Result: CRP方法使得自因模型在生成视频时对历史信息的依赖大幅减少，在受控实验中几乎达到了与双向模型相同的生成质量。基于CRP方法，作者训练了具有2000万参数的自因视频生成模型Optica，该模型能够自因生成5秒的480p视频，并在VBench上取得了82.78的高分，而训练视频数量仅为约1500万。

Conclusion: 本文提出并验证了CRP方法的有效性，能够显著提高自因模型的生成质量，尤其是在大规模参数条件下。此外，Optica模型展示了一种在自因模型中高效生成高质量视频的途径。

Abstract: Causal video diffusion models generate video autoregressively, which suits streaming, interactive, and long-video generation. Under standard training, however, they often yield lower generation quality than bidirectional models of the same size. Many existing approaches address this gap by initializing from or distilling a pretrained bidirectional teacher. We instead train a causal model from an image-model initialization, with no bidirectional video model at any stage. Because this path requires neither a large bidirectional teacher nor a complex distillation pipeline, it is simpler and more scalable. On this path, we find that a causal model trained on ground-truth history becomes strongly dependent on it, so that at inference errors in its own generated history propagate forward. We hypothesize that much of this dependence is unnecessary, because the current input already determines much of what the history provides. We propose Conditional Residual Prediction (CRP), a simple recipe for reducing a model's reliance on a condition: the model first predicts the target without the condition, and the condition may only add a residual on top of this prediction. Applied to history, CRP makes the model predict each chunk from the present as far as it can and use the past only for what the present cannot supply. In controlled experiments, CRP nearly closes the 6.14-point gap to a bidirectional model trained under the same setup. Scaling this recipe, we train Optica, a 2B-parameter causal video model that autoregressively generates 5-second 480p videos and reaches 82.78 on VBench with only about 15M training videos.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Resolution: Object-to-Image Ratio Mismatch in Instance Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11489)
*Boaz Meivar,Ofir Kedem,Amit Edenzon,Gal Chechik,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究表明，视觉实例检索中的失败主要由对象在两个不同图像中的占比不匹配引起，而不是分辨率损失。通过调整查询端尺度增强和使用OWLv2裁剪重排器，检索性能显著提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究背景和动机是为了解决视觉实例检索中物体在查询和画廊图像中不同尺寸的表现不佳问题。

Method: 通过在受控基准上使用3,021个Objaverse对象以五个不同的摄像机距离进行渲染，分析了不同拍档网络模型的表现；并通过规模增强和重排器提出了改进方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，针对ILIAS 100M数据集，未使用重排器前查询精度@1000为29.2，在使用OWLv2重排器后提升到42.0；并且利用LoRA微调也能达到类似的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结论表明，通过调整查询端的尺度增强和使用重排器，可以显著提高视觉实例检索的鲁棒性，这种方法无需训练或修改预计算的画廊索引。

Abstract: Visual instance retrieval often fails when the same object appears at different apparent sizes in the query and gallery. We show that the dominant cause is usually not resolution loss but object-to-image (O2I) ratio mismatch: the object occupies different fractions of the two images. On a controlled benchmark of 3,021 Objaverse objects rendered at five camera distances, more than 80% of the cross-distance degradation is attributable to O2I mismatch rather than resolution for 9 of 12 pretrained backbones; multi-scale architectures cut the resolution-only effect to single digits yet remain equally susceptible. The failure is also asymmetric: tight queries retrieve more reliably against wide gallery images than the reverse. Guided by this analysis, query-side scale augmentation and an OWLv2 crop reranker reach state of the art on ILIAS 100M (29.2 mAP@1000 before reranking, 42.0 after) without training or modifying the precomputed gallery index, and a LoRA fine-tune matches the query-side gains at a single forward pass, showing that O2I robustness is learnable.

</details>


### [83] [Stop My Dancing! Understanding, Detecting and Attributing Motion-Aware Deepfake Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11496)
*Fazhong Liu,Yan Meng,Tian Dong,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个针对运动感知深度换脸(MAD)的新基准和评估框架，发现了MAD视频中的高频伪影和模型特有的频谱指纹，并提出了MoDA防御框架进行检测和归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究针对新兴的MAD威胁进行分析，旨在了解其特征并提出有效的防御机制

Method: 研究首先创建了MAD特定的基准和度量框架，包含超过150万帧的视频，由六种可控生成器合成的1363个实际视频和30,122个合成视频组成，含有现实干扰，并进行开放世界评估分割。通过分析此数据集，提出了一种耦合空间语义与频域特征的MoDA防御框架。

Result: MoDA在内分布和跨数据集检测准确性上分别提高了10%至25%和91.5%的模型归因准确性。对于两个未见过的商业MAD平台生成的200个片段，MoDA达到了81.94%的准确率，对于开放互联网收集的1200个MAD视频片段的检测准确率为78.13%。MoDA在白盒、灰盒和黑盒的自适应攻击下保持了相对稳定的检测和归因性能。

Conclusion: 研究展示了MoDA框架在对抗MAD威胁方面的有效性和鲁棒性，对MAD进行有效检测和归因。

Abstract: Pose-guided diffusion models can now synthesize entire human figures in motion, spawning a new class of deepfakes: Motion Aware Deepfake (MAD) that have already reached hundreds of millions of viewers. To better understand this emerging threat, we construct the first MAD-specific benchmark and measurement framework, containing over 1.5 million frames that mix 1,363 real and 30,122 synthetic videos from six controllable generators, with realistic perturbations and open-world evaluation splits. Then, we dissect MAD and discover that, despite their global coherence, these videos betray faint yet reliable cues: because the model relies on limited input frames for motion synthesis, it must predict and simulate coherent movement at motion boundaries, thereby producing high-frequency artifacts along with model-specific spectral fingerprints. Based on the observations obtained from analysis on dataset, we propose MoDA, the first defense framework tailored to detect and attribute MAD videos. MoDA couples spatial semantics with steganalysis-rich frequency features via cross-domain alignment and multi-scale aggregation, achieving 94.8% in-distribution and 89.1% cross-dataset detection accuracy gains of 10% to 25% over prior work and 91.5% model attribution accuracy. MoDA achieves 81.94% accuracy on 200 clips produced by two unseen commercial MAD platforms, indicating promising zero-shot transfer, and 78.13% detection accuracy on 1,200 unseen MAD video clips (55k frames in total) collected from the open Internet. Under white-box, gray-box, and black-box adaptive attacks, MoDA maintains relatively stable detection and attribution performance while the accuracies of the baselines drop rapidly.

</details>


### [84] [Parametric Trajectory Distillation for Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11498)
*Lan Feng,Peter Karkus,Maximilian Igl,Julius Berner,Yuxiao Chen,Shuhan Tan,Alexandre Alahi,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Parametric Trajectory Distillation (PTD) 通过让学生参数化教师轨迹段并学习在其预测路径上的教师指导，显著提高了生成动态质量和自然度，特别是在大规模预训练模型上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法生成的轨迹曲线在高噪声下过于弯曲，超出学生能力范围并损害了细节，PTD 旨在通过适应后端预测能力的曲率调整，保持运动和多样性，以提高生成效果。

Method: PTD 方法允许学生将教师的轨迹段参数化为多项式，并通过教师在学生自预测路径上的指导进行学习。该方法在训练时采用曲率头部，而推断时保持原始后端架构不变。

Result: 在 Wan2.1-14B 模型上，四步 PTD 达到轨迹蒸馏的新标准，优于之前最好的轨迹方法 PDD。在 33B 音视频 MiniMax-H3 上，使用 LoRA 训练的 PTD 在多样性和自然度方面显著优于当前最佳方法 LightX2V Turbo。盲人投票显示，PTD 在对比 PDD 和 LightX2V Turbo 时分别获得了 55.1% 和 63.4% 的偏好比例。

Conclusion: PTD 提供了一种全新的轨迹蒸馏方法，能够在保持模型预测能力的同时优化生成质量，适用于大规模模型的高效生成任务。

Abstract: Video diffusion and flow models require many sequential evaluations, making generation computationally expensive. Few-step distillation reduces this cost but poses a capacity allocation problem: a student must match the teacher's iterative generation with far less sequential computation. Existing trajectory methods ask the student to reproduce teacher transitions that are highly curved at high noise, which can exceed its capacity and degrade fine detail. We introduce Parametric Trajectory Distillation (PTD), which lets the student parameterize teacher trajectory segments as polynomials and learn from teacher guidance along its own predicted path. PTD is designed to let the learned curvature adapt to the backbone's predictive capacity, preserving motion and diversity. The curvature head is used only in training; inference keeps the original backbone architecture. On Wan2.1-14B, four-step PTD sets a new state of the art for trajectory distillation, significantly improving dynamic quality and naturalness over PDD, the best-performing trajectory-only method on this model, under the same training setting. On the 33B audio-video MiniMax-H3, LoRA-trained PTD significantly improves diversity and naturalness over the state-of-the-art LightX2V Turbo. Blinded human votes give PTD 55.1% and 63.4% preference shares against PDD and LightX2V Turbo. Project page: https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/PTD/.

</details>


### [85] [MSGAT: Multi-Head Spiking Graph Attention with Similarity-Space Fusion for Image-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11526)
*Xintao Zong,Wenxuan Liu,Jianhao Ding,Zhaofei Yu,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种名为MSGAT的多头脉冲图形注意网络，并结合Sim-Fuse融合策略，在Flickr30K和MSCOCO数据集上取得了优于ANN方法和已有SNN检索基线的效果，同时节能超过55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNNs）由于其稀疏的事件驱动计算，对于高效的多模态学习具有巨大的潜力。然而，将其应用于高层多模态任务，如图像-文本检索（ITR），仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 文章提出了MSGAT，一种结合多头图形注意网络和动态注意头的多头脉冲图形注意网络。同时，引入了Sim-Fuse融合策略，以细粒度匹配方式融合粗粒度和细粒度关系，避免了直接融合异构表示的干扰。

Result: 在Flickr30K和MSCOCO数据集上，MSGAT及其Sim-Fuse融合策略在匹配设置下优于ANN方法，与现有的SNN检索基线相比也表现出更优或相当的性能，并且仅通过两步时间延迟实现了比其ANN对应物更好的性能，同时节省了55%的理论模块级能量。

Conclusion: 文章的新方法MSGAT及其Sim-Fuse融合策略提供了在特定多模态任务中提高SNN检索效率和效果的新解决方案，同时实现了能效的显著提升。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient computing paradigm through sparse event-driven computation, showing great potential for efficient multimodal learning. However, applying SNNs to high-level multimodal tasks, such as image-text retrieval (ITR), remains challenging, since sparse spike representations make it difficult to capture semantic structures required for cross-modal alignment. Existing spiking ITR methods rely on local alignment and additional soft-label supervision during training, while lacking awareness of structural and multi-granularity relationships. To address these issues, we propose a Multi-head Spiking Graph Attention Network (\textbf{MSGAT}) for structural modeling and equip it with dynamic attention heads to capture complementary relational patterns and enable spike-driven graph reasoning and aggregation. However, within a two-branch multi-granularity fusion framework, the fine-grained spike representations generated by MSGAT are sparse and discrete, whereas the global representations are continuous, making conventional feature-level fusion susceptible to interference across heterogeneous representations. Therefore, we introduce \textbf{Sim-Fuse}, a similarity-space fusion alignment strategy integrating coarse- and fine-grained matching relations while avoiding direct fusion of heterogeneous representations. Experiments on Flickr30K and MSCOCO show our method outperforms ANN methods under matched settings and existing SNN retrieval baselines. Moreover, with only two time steps, our SNN achieves comparable or superior performance to its ANN counterpart while reducing theoretical module-level energy by 55\%. The code is provided in the Supplementary Materials.

</details>


### [86] [HAND: A Biologically-Inspired Activation Function that Improves Generalisation and Sample Efficiency in Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11534)
*Michael W. Spratling,Heiko H. Schütt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种生物启发的激活函数HAND，通过实验表明，在图像分类任务中，该函数能使模型在更少的训练周期内达到与传统模型相当的精度，并且在数据减少或分布不均的情况下表现出更好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络(DNNs)在鲁棒性和泛化性方面不如人类，在学习过程中需要更多数据样本以达到相同效果。这种情况下，人为引入的归纳偏置可能帮助解决这些问题。

Method: 研究人员将生物启发的归纳偏置整合进一种新的激活函数HAND（Homeostasis, Accelerating Nonlinearity, and Divisive-nomalisation），并在ConvNeXt-tiny模型中进行了实验。

Result: 实验表明，使用HAND激活函数的ConvNeXt-tiny模型在ImageNet1k上的准确率与未修改模型在200个训练周期后的效果相同，仅需25个周期。此外，当训练数据量减少或分布不均时，这种激活函数的表现更为出色，并且不会随训练时间的增加而恶化。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，HAND可以在不牺牲样本效率的同时，减少所需的训练时间或数据量，从而提高样本的有效学习能力。这一结论具有广泛的应用前景，并且已在不同的CNN架构和数据集上得到了验证。

Abstract: DNNs exhibit robustness and generalisation issues not seen in humans. They are also far less data-efficient learners, requiring considerably more training samples to accurately classify novel exemplars. Inductive bias could help with these issues by providing in-built mechanisms to improve generalisation, and hence, reduce reliance on learning from data. We incorporate a biologically-inspired inductive bias into a new activation function, HAND (Homeostasis, Accelerating Nonlinearity, and Divisive-nomalisation), and show its effectiveness with CNNs trained on image classification. Using HAND a ConvNeXt-tiny required 25 training epochs to reach the same accuracy on ImageNet1k as the unmodified model achieved after 200 epochs. Consistent with the effects of an inductive bias, the performance gap reduced with training time and increased data augmentation. When the volume of training data was reduced and unevenly distributed between classes (Long-tailed ImageNet) the improvements in accuracy were even larger and did not reduce with increased training time. Generalisation performance with the common-corruptions data, and the ability to reject samples from unknown classes, were unaffected or improved by HAND. Results generalised across CNN architectures and training data-sets. HAND can, therefore, reduce the required training time and/or the required volume and variety of training data, helping to improve sample efficiency.

</details>


### [87] [OX-NeRF: 3D X-ray Tomography Reconstruction from Sparse Views Using Implicit Neural Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11547)
*Thomas Welsch,Min-Hsin Tu,David J. Chapman,Daniel E. Eakins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OX-NeRF 提出了一种新框架，结合跨场景特征学习和场景特定优化，以重建高度稀疏的 X 射线场景，相较于现有辐射场方法，提供了更高的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的3D重建方法难以处理仅有10个或更少视图的极稀疏场景，如高剂量率或低剂量采集的场景，而现有的神经辐射场方法在这里表现不佳。

Method: OX-NeRF 使用卷积神经网络识别跨场景特征，并保持位置特征的场景特定多分辨率哈希网格。配对表示被融合后传递给多层感知机，CNN、哈希网格和MLP共同端到端优化。

Result: 在并行束和锥束X射线数据集上的基准测试表明，OX-NeRF 在极稀疏场景下的重建准确性显著高于现有辐射场方法。

Conclusion: 因此，OX-NeRF 提供了一种有效的方法来处理高度稀疏的 X 射线场景。

Abstract: NeRF and Gaussian splatting methods have been successfully applied on X-ray scenes where the views are too sparse for 3D reconstruction via classical methods. Ultra-sparse scenes with 10 or fewer views such as those with high-rate or low-dose acquisition still, however, present a significant challenge. To address this problem we present a new framework, Optimised X-ray Neural Radiance Fields (OX-NeRF), that combines cross-scene feature learning with scene-specific optimisation to reconstruct sets of related scenes. OX-NeRF employs a convolutional neural network (CNN) to identify cross-scene features while maintaining scene-specific multi-resolution hash grids of spatial features. The paired representations are fused and passed to a multilayer perceptron (MLP); the CNN, hash grids and MLP are then jointly optimised end-to-end. Benchmarking on parallel-beam and cone-beam X-ray datasets shows OX-NeRF provides significantly higher reconstruction accuracy on ultra-sparse scenes compared to existing radiance field methods.

</details>


### [88] [Hankel Subspace Self-Supervised Learning for Parallel MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11560)
*Mingyu Hu,Siquan Zhu,Xijun Zhong,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HSSRecon是一种专为并行磁共振成像设计的重建框架，通过物理采集单元划分数据并在重叠窗口中应用多重归一化，网络学习紧凑的复值哈尔子空间算子，使用共轭梯度求解器在原始k空间变量上进行重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化并行磁共振成像的重建成像质量，通过利用多线圈和局部哈尔子空间的相关性，减少数据泄露，提高重建效果。

Method: HSSRecon通过物理采集单元划分数据并应用多重归一化，从哈尔子空间中学习紧凑的复杂值算子，利用共轭梯度求解器进行重建。

Result: 在快速MRI脑部数据集中，HSSRecon在六个综合条件中实现了与现有方法相当的峰值信噪比、结构相似性和归一化均方误差。

Conclusion: HSSRecon通过对多线圈和局部哈尔子空间相关信息的利用，为并行磁共振成像提供了有效的重建方法，表现出良好的重建效果。

Abstract: Parallel magnetic resonance imaging reconstruction is an ill-posed inverse problem under undersampling. Multi-coil acquisition and Hankel lifting expose complementary repeated information: observations of the same anatomy across coils and repeated local k-space neighborhoods in overlapping windows. These dependencies guide recovery of missing k-space data. However, splitting lifted Hankel entries for self-supervision can place the original sample in both input and target, causing data leakage. We propose Hankel Subspace Self-Supervised Reconstruction (HSSRecon), a scan-specific reconstruction framework for parallel magnetic resonance imaging. HSSRecon partitions data by physical acquisition units before Hankel lifting and applies multiplicity normalization to repeated Hankel copies in overlapping windows. Rather than learning a mapping that directly predicts missing data, the network learns a compact complex-valued Hankel subspace operator. Reconstruction is performed over the original k-space variables using a conjugategradient solver with hard data consistency. This design separates structural learning in the Hankel domain from data consistency in the physical domain: the former exploits multi-coil and local Hankel correlations, while the latter solves over unacquired degrees of freedom. We provide theoretical analyses of physicalgroup splitting and multiplicity normalization, and establish positive definiteness, uniqueness, hard data consistency, and a finite-step conjugate-gradient error bound for the system. On fastMRI brain data with three contrasts and three sampling masks, HSSRecon achieves competitive peak signal-to-noise ratio, structural similarity, and normalized mean squared error across six aggregated conditions.

</details>


### [89] [PAM-ToD: Plug-and-Play Appearance Modeling for Cross-Time-of-Day 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11572)
*Kota Shimomura,Sungho Moon,Tsubasa Hirakawa,Takayoshi Yamashita,Sunghoon Im,Hironobu Fujiyoshi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAM-ToD 是一种轻量级的插件，可以利用少量的锚定点图像来适应预训练的 3DGS 路景模型到新的时间段，同时保持光照参数不变，能够较好地实现真实感实时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不同的时间段，道路场景的外观会有所变化，如光照和颜色。然而，直接适应预训练模型可能导致显著的不一致或需要大量的训练数据。本研究旨在开发一种能用少量锚定点图像来适应不同时间段的解决方案，同时保持模型性能。

Method: PAM-ToD 方法采用简化图像形成模型，通过调整高斯的现有颜色来模拟照明变化，并利用加性项来适应亮度变化。它避免了直接估计反照率和照明的影响，利用锚定点图像学习颜色校正，同时在空间变化上施加限制。

Result: PAM-ToD 在静态和动态设置下的表现优于基线模型，具体表现为更高的 PSNR（峰值信噪比）和更低的 LPIPS（感知相似性度量）。即使锚定点图像来自单一同步捕获的多摄像头，也能取得理想的结果。

Conclusion: PAM-ToD 是一种有效的方法，可在使用少量锚定点图像的情况下实时调整 3DGS 路景模型，并实现高质量的外观变化，适用于不同的时间段应用。

Abstract: Adapting a pre-trained 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) road scene to a new time of day requires learning appearance changes from a few anchor images while preserving consistent, real-time rendering. We propose PAM-ToD, a lightweight plug-in that learns color corrections while keeping the pre-trained 3DGS parameters fixed. PAM-ToD scales each Gaussian's existing color to model illumination changes and uses an additive term for additional brightness, such as when street lamps turn on at night. Under a simplified image formation model, unchanged surface albedo can be eliminated from the relation between source and target appearances, allowing us to learn these corrections without separately estimating albedo and illumination. The model corrects colors across the scene while allowing the corrections to vary by location and by Gaussian. To guide learning from a few anchor images, it discourages abrupt spatial changes in these corrections. We also introduce CARLA-ToD, a benchmark with matching geometry, camera poses, and moving-object trajectories across three times of day. A few target-time anchor images are used to train each plug-in, while separate views are used for evaluation. Across the static and dynamic settings, PAM-ToD achieves higher PSNR and lower LPIPS than the baselines, even when the anchor images come from a single synchronized capture across multiple cameras.

</details>


### [90] [SV-TAD: Native Sparse Convs for Efficient Temporal Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11579)
*Ricardo Pizarro,Roberto Valle,José M. Buenaposada,Luis M. Bergasa,Luis Baumela*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为native sparse 2D卷积的技术，使适配器能够直接且高效地在动态剪裁的token集合上操作，从而减少了VideoMAEv2-L的计算量高达64%，使推理速度提升2.2倍，同时保持了THUMOS-14和ActivityNet-1.3上的最佳准确率。在InternVideoNext-L扩展时，该方法的计算成本仅为以前方法的一半左右，且辅助任务标记自然支持，进一步提升了细粒度组装检测的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽然有效提升了参数效率，但在推理时间计算上没有得到优化，且Token选择技术会破坏卷积适配器所需的网格结构，导致密集重建，无法有效加速。

Method: 研究引入了一种名为native sparse 2D卷积的新技术，允许适配器在动态剪裁的token集合上直接且高效地操作，改变了传统方法中密集重建带来的高计算成本。

Result: 该方法将VideoMAEv2-L的计算量减少了64%，实现了2.2倍的快速推理，同时在THUMOS-14和ActivityNet-1.3上保持了最佳准确率，并在使用InternVideoNext-L时，计算成本降低了一半。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法显著提高了Vision Transformers在长视频理解中的计算效率和推理速度，同时保持了准确性，并支持辅助任务标记，为该领域的技术进步做出了贡献。

Abstract: To adapt billion-parameter Vision Transformers for long-video understanding, recent methods freeze the backbone and train lightweight convolutional modules. While effective for parameter-efficient training, existing adapters do not reduce inference-time computation, leaving scalability with respect to video length largely unaddressed. Token selection can reduce attention cost by pruning redundant tokens, but it breaks the spatial grid structure required by convolutional adapters. This forces an expensive dense reconstruction, nullifying much of the potential speedup. We address this by introducing native sparse 2D convolutions, a primitive that allows these adapters, for the first time, to operate directly and efficiently on dynamically pruned token sets. We integrate this primitive into SV-TAD, an adapter framework for temporal action detection, reducing VideoMAEv2-L computation by up to 64% and achieving 2.2x faster inference, while maintaining state-of-the-art accuracy on THUMOS-14 and ActivityNet-1.3. When scaled to InternVideoNext-L, our approach surpasses the previous state of the art at roughly half its computational cost. Moreover, the sparse formulation naturally supports auxiliary task tokens, which improves fine-grained assembly detection on ATTACH.

</details>


### [91] [S$^3$Geo: Structure-Semantic Synergistic Learning for Cross-View Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11608)
*Ziqian Mo,Hill Zhang,Haosheng Tan,Ling Li,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S$^3$Geo 提出了一种协同学习框架，通过结合结构和语义信息来解决跨视角图像匹配中的局部结构模式建模和视空间错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖于视觉表示，但在处理大视角变化时容易混淆语义不同但视觉相似的区域，导致鲁棒性不足。因此，为了提出一种新的方法来解决这些问题。

Method: S$^3$Geo 方法采用了解耦查询池化模块、查询级对比学习方案以及语义知识蒸馏策略，以建立软对应关系并进行语义先验和关系结构的转移。

Result: 实验结果表明，S$^3$Geo 在 University-1652 和 SUES-200 数据集上的表现优于现有方法，而无需增加推理复杂度。

Conclusion: 结合结构和语义信息对于跨视角图像匹配具有显著的提升效果，S$^3$Geo 方法表明了这种方法的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization (CVGL) aims to estimate geographic locations by matching images captured from different viewpoints, such as drone and satellite views. Existing methods mainly rely on visual representations, but often fail to jointly model fine-grained structural correspondences and semantic priors, making them prone to confusion between visually similar but semantically different regions, and thus limiting robustness under large viewpoint variations. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{S$^3$Geo}, a structure-semantic synergistic learning framework for cross-view matching. Specifically, we first introduce a Decoupled Query Pooling (DQP) module to extract a compact set of region-aware features from dense tokens, enabling explicit modeling of local structural patterns. We then design a query-level contrastive learning scheme with an optimal transport (OT)-based formulation to establish soft correspondences under cross-view spatial misalignment. Furthermore, we incorporate a Semantic Knowledge Distillation (SKD) strategy from a frozen CLIP teacher to transfer semantic priors and relational structures, thereby improving discrimination on hard negatives. By operating synergistically, the semantic priors provide robust contextual filtering, which guides the structural module to establish precise spatial alignments. Experiments on the University-1652 and SUES-200 datasets demonstrate that \textbf{S$^3$Geo} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches without increasing inference complexity, validating the effectiveness of jointly modeling structural and semantic information for CVGL.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond Report Imitation: Clinically Aware Multi-Image Ultrasound Report Generation from Visible Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11610)
*Yuchen Yang,Xin Wang,Lufan Wang,Yinghong Pan,Yujuan Feng,Yuqing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAMEO框架通过三个阶段的学习和调整，解决多幅图像生成超声报告时需要综合临床证据的问题，显著提高了报告的质量和临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声图像是动态的，但存档的关键帧只能捕捉到部分动态检查，导致从图像中无法验证的内容可能是临床有效的。这一差距在指导模型生成报告时造成了对临床行为的不一致。由此提出了CAMEO框架，以解决这个问题。

Method: CAMEO旨在解决这一临床行为不一致的问题。该框架分为三个阶段：第一阶段学习超声视觉语言的元素；第二阶段进行跨视图证据整合；第三阶段进行临床意识偏好对齐。

Result: 实验结果表明，CAMEO框架在USReport-Distilled基准上优于EchoVLM，BLEU-1从0.25提高到0.40，ROUGE-1从0.28提高到0.45，METEOR从0.27提高到0.43，临床评分提高了29.18%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于证据的监督、临床意识的对齐以及临床结构化的评估对于生成可靠的超声报告非常重要。

Abstract: Generating ultrasound reports from multiple images requires aggregating clinical evidence across views, yet archived key frames capture only part of the dynamic examination. Raw-report imitation is therefore misaligned with visual supervision: content that is clinically valid for the full examination may be unverifiable from the images available to a model. This gap creates a clinical behavior alignment problem. A model must preserve visible findings, avoid diagnostic reversals and unsupported completion, and not collapse into conservative templates. We propose CAMEO, a Clinically Aware Multi-image Evidence-grounded Orchestration framework for ultrasound report generation. Stage I learns ultrasound visual-language primitives; Stage II performs Cross-View Evidence Grounding by distilling trusted visible report points into multi-image QA and report-style supervision; and Stage III performs Clinically Aware Preference Alignment using clinical-error-oriented preference pairs. From USReport, we construct USReport-Distilled with 17,670 evidence-grounded paired-image training instances and USReport-Pref with 21,869 preference pairs; we additionally use 25,631 PubMedVision-US ultrasound instruction samples for domain adaptation and multi-image instruction tuning. On the primary USReport-Distilled benchmark, CAMEO improves over EchoVLM from 0.25 to 0.40 BLEU-1, 0.28 to 0.45 ROUGE-1, and 0.27 to 0.43 METEOR, while raising ClinicalScore from 55.02 to 74.20. These results underscore the value of evidence-grounded supervision, clinically aware alignment, and clinically structured evaluation for reliable ultrasound report generation.

</details>


### [93] [PointVGGT: Zero-Shot Multiview RGB-D Point Cloud Registration with Visual Geometry Foundation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11612)
*Haobo Jiang,Liang Yu,Jianmin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉几何基模型（VGGT）的无监督多视角RGB-D配准方法，命名为PointVGGT，该方法通过两个阶段实现：基础阶段使用基础模型直接获得全局一致的姿态，不依赖于对偶估计；优化阶段通过高效的体素化空间哈希机制和迭代加权最小二乘法进行密集多视角配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有方法在局部优化对偶配准、严重误差传播和高计算负担方面的不足，本文提出了一种基于视觉几何基模型（VGGT）的Zero-Shot框架，用于改善多视角RGB-D配准的鲁棒性和计算效率。

Method: PointVGGT采用‘基础-优化’的新范式，首先使用基础模型直接估计全局一致的姿态，然后利用高效的空间体素化哈希方法和迭代加权最小二乘进行优化，实现多视角配准。

Result: 在室内、物体中心和室外数据集上的实验表明，与现有方法相比，PointVGGT方法在零样本配准时表现出卓越的准确性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法提供了一种用于多视角RGB-D配准的有效且计算效率高的解决方案，特别是在计算资源有限的应用场景下。

Abstract: This paper addresses multiview RGB-D point cloud registration, aiming to estimate global rigid poses for unordered RGB-D scans and align them in a metrically consistent coordinate frame. The conventional pairwise-then-global paradigm suffers from locally optimized pairwise registration, severe error propagation and high computational burden. In particular, existing methods typically treat RGB data as a mere auxiliary matching cue and overlook the holistic geometric priors (e.g., camera poses and 3D models) encoded across image sequences. This paper introduces PointVGGT, a zero-shot framework built upon a novel \emph{foundation-then-refinement} paradigm that systematically leverages visual geometry foundation models (e.g., VGGT) as the computational backbone for robust, training-free multiview RGB-D registration. In the foundation stage, we directly recover metrically consistent global poses (without any pairwise estimation) by grounding the scale-ambiguous pose predictions of the foundation model against metric depth observations. In the refinement stage, we introduce an efficient voxelized spatial hashing mechanism that exploits the globally coherent 3D reconstruction (induced by the foundation model) as a shared spatial anchor, enabling dense multiview correspondences in near-linear time. On top of this, an IRLS-based robust motion-only bundle adjustment is performed using a conjugate gradient solver to jointly minimize the correspondence and reprojection residuals for multiview pose refinement. Extensive experiments on indoor/object-centric/outdoor datasets verify the outstanding zero-shot registration accuracy and computational efficiency of our proposed method.

</details>


### [94] [TAM: Task-Aware Memory Distillation for Efficient Spatiotemporal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11617)
*Yuqi Li,Xiaoqin Feng,Fan Xu,Weilun Feng,Chuanguang Yang,Yingli Tian,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAM（任务感知记忆蒸馏）是一种任务感知的记忆蒸馏框架，通过将冻结教师的知识组织为可检索的历史记忆，有效利用跨样本预测结构，提升模型在视频预测、天气预报和交通流预测等任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法在处理每个样本独立匹配输出或特征时，未能有效利用跨样本的预测结构。TAM通过利用任务特定的历史参考，改进了这一问题。

Method: TAM框架包括记忆（Memory）模块以及指导学生学习的知识的存储和检索机制。记忆模块中的条目存储了潜在特征、预测变化或流动残差，任务特定的选择规则会识别出相关的历史参考。学生层采用与教师相似性分布匹配或观察条件残差原型回归的方式学习。

Result: TAM在视频预测、天气预报和交通流预测任务上进行了评估，相对于常规的知识蒸馏基线，提高了SSIM指标并降低了MSE。具体来说，在KittiCaltech、WeatherBench（使用gSTA教师）和TaxiBJ上的平均MSE减少了1.86%、1.93%和1.01%。

Conclusion: TAM框架在多个任务上的成功应用表明，通过历史教师监督任务，可以有效提升预测任务的性能，而无需额外增加学生的推理成本。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation enables efficient spatiotemporal prediction by transferring knowledge from an accurate teacher to a compact student. However, matching outputs or features independently for each sample leaves cross-sample predictive structure underused. Exploiting this structure requires representations and historical references that reflect the dynamics of each task. We propose TAM, a Task-Aware Memory Distillation framework that organizes a frozen teacher's knowledge into a bounded, retrievable history. Memory entries encode latent features, forecast changes, or flow residuals, while task-specific selection rules identify relevant historical references. The student either matches the teacher's similarity distribution over shared references or regresses observation-conditioned residual prototypes. These objectives complement supervised prediction and conventional distillation. The teacher, memory, and auxiliary adapters are used only during training, leaving student inference unchanged. We evaluate TAM on video prediction, weather forecasting, and traffic flow prediction across multiple teacher-student configurations. Averaged over four paired runs, adding TAM improves SSIM on all six video datasets and reduces MSE on five relative to the corresponding KD baselines. Mean paired MSE reductions reach 1.86% on KittiCaltech, 1.93% on WeatherBench with a gSTA teacher, and 1.01% on TaxiBJ. These results demonstrate the utility of historical teacher supervision across distinct forecasting tasks without additional student inference cost.

</details>


### [95] [Revisiting Handcrafted Minutiae Detection: A Simple and Effective Open Source Baseline for Modern Fingerprint Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11641)
*Raffaele Cappelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SBMEX 是一种加速的手工制作特征点提取方法，通过使用查找表架构和连续质量评分框架提高了计算效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源的传统基线算法过时，缺乏与现代科学软件生态系统的无缝集成。SBMEX 旨在为特征点检测任务提供一种快速且确定性的解决方案。

Method: SBMEX 使用双查找表架构来优化交叉数计算和骨架跟踪过程，并结合连续质量评分框架，通过跟踪路径长度、双脊谷骨架融合和空间密度衰减来提升特征提取的准确性。

Result: SBMEX 在 NIST SD302 数据集上的评估表明，其特征提取的准确性和传统开源基线相当或更优，同时极大地减少了特征点检测的延迟，特别是相较于传统的 Python 实现。

Conclusion: SBMEX 作为一种高效的手工特征点提取方法，已在开源项目 pyfing 中集成，能够满足生物识别科学和法医实践中的需求。

Abstract: Handcrafted minutiae detection algorithms remain fundamental to biometric science and forensic practice due to their full auditability, adherence to international standards, and operational independence from training datasets or GPU hardware. However, current open-source traditional baselines are severely outdated, relying almost exclusively on legacy C/C++ codebases that lack seamless integration with modern scientific software ecosystems. To bridge this gap, the present work introduces SBMEX (Skeleton-Based Minutiae EXtraction), a fast and deterministic minutiae detection method integrated into the open source \texttt{pyfing} package. SBMEX achieves high computational throughput by employing a dual Look-Up Table architecture that replaces runtime neighborhood scanning during Crossing Number computation and skeleton tracking. Additionally, it incorporates a continuous quality scoring framework driven by tracking path length, dual ridge-valley skeleton fusion, and spatial density decay. Rigorous evaluation on NIST SD302 datasets demonstrates that SBMEX delivers feature extraction accuracy comparable to or outperforming traditional open-source baselines without fine-tuning, while achieving a drastic reduction in minutiae detection latency relative to classical Crossing Number Python implementations.

</details>


### [96] [DisFace3DNet: Explainable Facial Attractiveness Prediction via 3D Component Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11656)
*Fenggui Rao,Yan Luximon,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisFace3DNet 使用 3D 组件解纠缠方法，通过辅助弱语义监督从总体评分中学习七个组件的参考分数，无需人工标记的组件目标。该方法将五种静态评分和两种动态评分结合成总体评分，揭示了每个组件的数值贡献，并支持图像间的组件特定比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的面部吸引力预测方法通常给一个整体评分，而忽视了形状、外观和观看条件的作用。本文旨在通过一种新的3D组件解纠缠方法，将这些因素量化并明确地反映在评分中。

Method: DisFace3DNet 方法利用3D表示和图像线索训练不同的渠道来学习面部身份、皮肤、头发、照明、背景、表情和姿势这七个组成部分的参考评分。然后使用一种受限的拟合方法将五个静态评分和两个动态评分综合成一个总体评分。

Result: 在 SCUT-FBP5500 数据集上，DisFace3DNet 达到了与人类评分相关系数为0.8904±0.0063（五折平均及其标准差），其组件项能够再现出每一项留出的预测值。皮肤、头发和面部形状是预测变异最大的部分。

Conclusion: DisFace3DNet 将面部预测与其组成部分（如面部和环境线索）的定量分析相结合，支持面部特征的具体比较。

Abstract: Facial attractiveness prediction usually assigns one overall rating, leaving the roles of shape, appearance, and viewing conditions implicit. We propose DisFace3DNet, which uses 3D component disentanglement to learn seven component reference scores from overall ratings with auxiliary weak semantic supervision, without human-labeled component targets. Designated 3D representations and image cues feed jointly learned routes for identity, skin, hair, light, background, expression, and pose. A constrained fit then combines five static and two signed dynamic scores into the overall rating, exposing each component's numerical contribution and supporting component-specific comparisons across images. On SCUT-FBP5500, DisFace3DNet achieves a Pearson correlation of $0.8904\pm0.0063$ (mean $\pm$ standard deviation across five folds) with average human ratings; its component terms reconstruct every held-out prediction to numerical precision. Skin, hair, and facial shape account for the largest component-wise prediction variation. Human evaluation supports the score directions for facial shape, skin, and hair; expression agreement is weaker. DisFace3DNet thus connects overall prediction to quantitative analysis of the facial and contextual cues entering each estimate.

</details>


### [97] [Perceptually Grounded and Semantics-Aware Evaluation for Holistic Co-Speech Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11669)
*Nick Milkin,Lanmiao Liu,Esam Ghaleb,Asli Ozyurek,Zerrin Yumak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个基于感知和语义感知的基准，涵盖了13个客观指标，并提出了一种新的语义感知度量方法，通过参与者评分和目标特定综合度量改进了感知一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客观度量标准不能始终如一地反映人类感知，语义适宜性难以量化。因此，研究旨在创建一个基准，结合模型对比、人类中心的指标验证和细粒度语义评估。

Method: 研究首先整理了涵盖不同方面的13个客观度量标准，包括分布相似性、几何保真度、动态质量、跨模态同步性和语义适宜性。对于语义适宜性类别，提出了一个新的度量方法，称为语义手势保持度量。然后进行了一项感知研究，由101名参与者根据人性化、多样性、动画错误的缺失、语音同步和内容匹配等五个维度对生成的手势进行评分。研究对客观度量标准与主观评分之间的关联进行了系统分析。

Result: 结果发现，语义得分与感知维度之间不存在显著相关性，而语义手势保持度量则与语音感知的人类判断相一致。构建了五个目标特定的综合度量，以改进所有五维度的感知一致性，特别是在避免动画错误和内容匹配方面。

Conclusion: 客观度量标准需要与主观评估进行验证，以更好地反映人类判断。

Abstract: Holistic and semantics-aware co-speech gesture generation has advanced rapidly, yet evaluation remains behind: objective metrics do not consistently reflect human perception, and semantic appropriateness remains difficult to quantify. We present a perceptually grounded and semantics-aware benchmark that combines standardized model comparison, human-centered metric validation, and fine-grained semantic evaluation. We first curate a list of 13 objective metrics covering different aspects, including distributional similarity, geometric fidelity, kinematic quality, cross-modal synchrony, and semantic appropriateness. For the semantic-appropriateness category, we propose a new metric, Semantic Gesture Preservation (SGP), which measures how far semantic gestures in the ground truth are preserved in the generated gestures. For this, we augment the BEAT2 dataset's annotations using a multi-modal LLM. We then conduct a perceptual study where 101 participants score generated gestures among five dimensions, including human-likeness, motion diversity, absence of animation errors, speech timing and content match. We systematically analyze objective metric--subjective score correlations. Unlike Semantic Score (SC), which shows no significant association with the evaluated perceptual dimensions, SGP is selectively aligned with speech-aware human judgments. We construct five target-specific composite metrics aligned with the subjective dimensions. These composites improve perceptual alignment across all five dimensions, with the largest gains for absence of animation errors and content match, indicating that complementary objective signals can better approximate human judgments than individual metrics alone. Overall, our results show that objective metrics require validation against subjective evaluations.

</details>


### [98] [VESSI - VLM-Enhanced Support for Surveillance and Investigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11674)
*Saverio Cavasin,Pietro Tedeschi,Mattia Tamiazzo,Alessandro Brighente,Simone Milani,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的增强视频监控分析框架VESSI，通过文本描述关键视觉特征，提升自动视频监控分析能力，减少人工审查时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视频监控系统缺乏语义模块，无法提供全面的态势感知和法医任务支持，限制了它们的事件理解和后事件调查能力，导致操作效率低下，增加了人工分析师的负担。

Method: 研究采用了基于视觉语言模型的方法，将视频中的重要视觉特征转换为文本描述，通过提示驱动的方式进行视频序列的查询，同时提出了无参考模型性能评估方法CMUS来评估VLM性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，该解决方案极大提高了人工操作者的分析能力，并增强了自动监控系统的灵活性。最可靠的模型在超过66%的视频中识别出潜在关联活动，并将审查时间减少了超过85%，在选择性和效率之间达到了平衡。无参考CMUS评估与标准视频CMUS评估结果一致，与5,909个手动参考帧获得的假阳性率排序相符，支持评估分数在评估环境下的操作使用。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的VESSI框架在增强自动视频监控分析方面的实践应用中表现出了良好的效果，为未来的研究和应用提供了有参考价值的路径。

Abstract: Automated video surveillance analysis has become a critical component of intelligence infrastructures and Law Enforcement agencies. Traditional systems lack the semantic module for comprehensive situational awareness and forensic tasks, limiting their ability to interpret events meaningfully or support post-incident investigations. This slows operational insight and increases the burden on human analysts. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer promising pathways to bridge this gap. To address this, we propose VLM-Enhanced Support for Surveillance and Investigations (VESSI), a VLM-based framework designed to enhance automated video surveillance analysis through prompt-driven interrogation of video sequences where salient visual features are converted into textual descriptions. We test our framework with four state-of-the-art models. Since most datasets for this task are unlabeled, we also propose the Composite Model Utility Score (CMUS) to assess VLM performance. Experimental results show that our solution substantially improves the analysis capabilities of human operators and enhances the flexibility of automated surveillance systems. In our evaluation, the most reliable model flagged potentially relevant activity in more than 66% of the videos while reducing review time by more than 85%, offering a practical balance between selectivity and efficiency. The model ordering produced by the reference-free CMUS evaluation was reproduced by the normal-video CMUS evaluation and matched the false-positive-rate ordering obtained from 5,909 manually referenced frames. This agreement supports the operational use of the score within the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [99] [HI3D 3.0 (Twinkle3D): Object-specific 3D Asset Generation with High Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11685)
*Ziying Li,Shengchu Zhao,Huiang He,Yiyang Chen,Jianwen Huang,Bailin Li,Changhao Li,Jianhui Li,Jie Li,Ruiyang Liu,Yibo Luo,Tengjiao Sun,Pei Tang,Shiwen Wang,Jiaqi Wu,Kang Wu,Kaiqiao Yang,Zherui Yang,Hu Zhang,Xuezhi Zhao,Xinhe Zheng,Yukun Li,Heliang Zheng,Rongfei Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hi3D 3.0引入了一种高性能的图像到3D生成系统，通过先进的几何模型Twinkle3D生成高保真度的三角形网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决图像到3D生成过程中物体特定细节的保真度问题，特别是保留物体的关键几何结构如标记和重复结构。

Method: 通过Twinkle3D模型改进高保真几何生成，利用重新设计的DiT架构和大规模分布式训练优化，提高几何生成质量和效率。

Result: Hi3D 3.0在几何保真度评估中表现优异，超过4种商业系统，恢复笔迹字符达到82.1%，准确率达到98.2%，显著提高了物体特定结构的恢复。

Conclusion: 证明Twinkle3D模型在保持高精度的同时，能有效生成高质量的封闭三角形网格。

Abstract: Image-to-3D generation has become increasingly capable of producing objects that closely resemble the input image, and an outstanding challenge is to reproduce the depicted object itself, including the specific geometry that defines it. Inscriptions, brand marks, and repeated structures are frequently distorted or lost, despite being critical to object identity. We present Hi3D 3.0, an image-to-3D generation system targeting object-specific fidelity, with Twinkle3D as its geometry model for generating watertight triangle meshes at $2048^{3}$ resolution. Twinkle3D advances high-fidelity geometry generation along four dimensions. First, while O-Voxel/FaithC offers high representational precision, it often suffers from poor surface quality and non-watertight geometry. We address both issues while retaining its $2048^{3}$-level precision. Second, we scale diffusion generation to sequences of up to 300K geometric tokens through a redesigned DiT architecture and large-scale distributed training optimizations, reducing training time per step from approximately ten minutes to ten seconds. Third, subsequent refinement cannot fully compensate for errors introduced during initial generation; we therefore strengthen both global shape and local detail in the initial generation stage, and the resulting single-stage model surpasses prior two-stage pipelines with $512^{3}$ refinement. Finally, we introduce a fine-grained image-3D cross-modal interaction mechanism that strengthens correspondence between visual evidence and geometric tokens, improving the recovery of object-specific structures. We evaluate geometric fidelity using alignment metrics derived from silhouettes and normal fields. Hi3D 3.0 outperforms four commercial systems across all reported metrics, recovering 82.1% of inscribed characters at 98.2% precision, compared with 21.7% recall for the strongest competitor.

</details>


### [100] [Towards Unified Evaluation of Prompt Enhancers for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11736)
*Yawen Shao,Yubo Zhu,Ziyun Dai,Zixun Fang,Kai Zhu,Zeyinzi Jiang,Yufeng Ai,Siyang Sun,Haolan Xue,Yu Shang,Yuxiang Bao,Zoubin Bi,Jingming Luo,Jie Xiao,Chaojie Mao,Zhehan Kan,Hongchen Luo,Yu Liu,Sheng Zhong,Wei Tong,Xueyang Fu,Yang Cao,Wei Zhai,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了PEBench，一个用于直接评估提示增强器的基准，涵盖各类视频生成任务，并通过证据基评估框架揭示了现有提示增强技术的发展趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示增强器评估方法依赖于渲染视频，这种方法耗时且成本高，影响了提示增强器的训练和迭代，同时混淆了提示增强器的质量和下游生成器的行为。因此，为了填补这一空白，研究者提出了PEBench这一基准，旨在直接评估提示增强器的性能。

Method: PEBench汇集了1,100个专家验证案例和1,005个视觉资产，涵盖了35个详细的子任务，利用模态感知的事实提取与评分标准评估相结合的方法，评估了代表性的开放源和闭源提示增强器。

Result: 实验结果显示，提示增强技术正从细粒度的描述性扩展转向保留意图的电影级规划，而图像重建和前景细化方法在电影覆盖度和语义准确性或内部一致性方面分别具有互补优势。PEBench的评分与Wan3.0和MiniMax-H3改进后的文本和视频的专家判断高度一致，表明提示级评估能更好地反映下游应用的效果。

Conclusion: PEBench为直接评估提示增强器提供了统一的方法，有助于指导该领域的技术进步和发展。

Abstract: Modern video generators can realize increasingly complex visual narratives, positioning the prompt enhancer (PE) as a critical bridge from concise user instructions and multimodal references to structured cinematic plans. However, existing PE evaluation relies on rendered videos, imposing substantial computational and human costs, slowing PE training and iteration, and conflating PE quality with downstream generator behavior. To address this gap, we introduce PEBench, the first unified benchmark for direct PE evaluation across text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video prompt enhancement. It comprises 1,100 expert-verified cases and 1,005 visual assets, spanning 35 fine-grained tasks with diverse temporal, cinematic, audiovisual, and multi-reference requirements. In addition, we develop PEBench evaluation, an evidence-grounded framework that combines modality-aware fact extraction with rubric-based assessment across 24 criteria. Our systematic evaluation of representative open- and closed-source PE methods reveals an emerging shift from fine-grained descriptive expansion toward intent-preserving cinematic planning, while the caption-reconstruction and forward-refinement methods show complementary strengths in cinematic coverage and semantic fidelity or internal coherence, respectively. Human validation shows that PEBench scores align closely with expert judgments of enhanced prompts and downstream videos from Wan3.0 and MiniMax-H3, indicating that prompt-level evaluation reliably reflects downstream utility.

</details>


### [101] [Streaming-Aware Diffusion for Real-Time Video Super-Resolution via Cross-Step Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11746)
*Harris Partaourides,Sotirios Chatzis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种流式感知框架，将预训练的单帧隐空间扩散模型应用于视频超分辨率（VSR），通过跨步注意力机制和轨迹耦合扩散调度，提高了计算效率并保持了帧内稳定性和时间现实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频超分辨率需要在严格的延迟约束下提供高空间-时间保真度，这对于基于迭代采样的扩散模型提出了挑战，因为它们的计算成本很高且缺乏时间协调。作者提出了一种框架，通过利用视频流的序列结构来提升预训练模型的效率。

Method: 提出了跨步注意力机制和轨迹耦合扩散调度，优化了视频流中的帧间和帧内处理，从而在保持计算效率的同时提高时间连贯性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在RES4和YouHQ40-Test数据集上在感知质量和时间现实性方面均有所提升，且在512×512分辨率下实现了超过40 FPS的帧率，实现无显式时间建模的实时视频超分辨率。

Conclusion: 作者所提出的方法在计算效率和时间连贯性方面取得了显著的改进，为实时视频超分辨率应用提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time video super-resolution requires high spatio-temporal fidelity under strict latency constraints, challenging diffusion models due to their iterative sampling cost and limited temporal coordination. We propose a streaming-aware framework that adapts pretrained single-image latent diffusion models for efficient video super-resolution (VSR) by exploiting the sequential structure of video streams. Our Cross-Step Attention mechanism reuses intermediate denoising features across adjacent frames and diffusion steps, enabling temporal information exchange without explicit temporal modeling. We further introduce Trajectory-Coupled Diffusion Scheduling, which aligns adjacent diffusion states and provides cleaner intermediate representations for cross-step conditioning, improving temporal coherence. These components are integrated into a streaming inference pipeline that incrementally propagates latent states across frames, reducing the effective computational complexity from $O(N \cdot S)$ to $O(N + S)$ for $N$ frames and $S$ diffusion steps. Experiments on REDS4 and YouHQ40-Test demonstrate improved perceptual quality and temporal realism while maintaining frame-wise stability. Our method achieves over 40 FPS at $512 \times 512$ resolution after cold start, enabling real-time VSR without explicit temporal modeling.

</details>


### [102] [Dino Forcing Flow Models: Do not denoise what you can predict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11751)
*Arijit Ghosh,Lucas Degeorge,Paul Couairon,Alexei A Efros,Vicky Kalogeiton,David Picard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种直接预测预训练表征并基于预测条件的方法，以替换第二个去噪轨迹，加快训练速度并提高生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的co-denoising方法虽然能提高模型训练速度和质量，但需要设计新的去噪安排，因此我们提出了一个更简单的解决方案。

Method: 直接预测预训练表征，然后再基于该预测条件训练模型。

Result: 该方法比现有方法在ImageNet上快两倍的训练周期内表现更佳，并在像素空间中提高了FID分数超过20%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，预测而非去噪可以提高训练速度和生成质量。

Abstract: Co-denoising pretrained representations such as DINO can substantially improve the training speed and quality of flow matching models, but it introduces a second denoising trajectory and requires carefully designed schedules. We propose a simpler alternative: predict the pretrained representation directly, then condition the model on its own prediction. This removes the need for a second ODE and any representation-specific denoising schedules, while retaining the benefits of representation guidance. Our approach converges substantially faster and achieves better generation quality as measured by FID score. On ImageNet, it outperforms the state of the art in latent space at 2x fewer epochs than prior methods; in pixel space, it improves FID over comparable prior methods by more than 20%. These results support a simple principle: do not denoise what you can predict. Our code is openly available at https://github.com/arijit-hub/dino_forcing.

</details>


### [103] [DataVista: Diagnosing Multimodal LLMs on Data Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11993)
*Yupeng Xie,Zhenyang Wang,Jiayi Zhu,Yinghao Tang,Zhouan Shen,Yiyu Chen,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了DataVista基准，这是首个专门针对数据视频理解的数据集，涵盖了数据感知、时间推理和叙事理解三个层次，共包含961个真实数据视频及其6775个评估问题，并分析了主流MLLMs在该基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据视频理解基准仅涵盖了一般视频或静态图表，导致缺乏系统性评估。因此，研究者提出了DataVista基准，以填补这一空白。

Method: 研究者构建了一个包含961个真实数据视频的大型数据集，并设计了一个三级渐进能力框架（数据感知、时间推理、叙事理解）来组织6775个评估问题，还详细分析了模型的响应以识别典型的失败模式。

Result: 主流MLLMs在DataVista基准上的表现不佳，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro的整体准确率为70.0%，远低于人类专家水平。模型在因果推理和叙事结构方面表现最差。增加帧数和添加字幕对数据感知和时间推理有益，但对叙事理解的提升有限。

Conclusion: 研究团队通过DataVista基准提出了关于数据视频理解的挑战，并展示了目前主要模型的局限性，为该领域未来的研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Data video is a media form that integrates data visualization with video narrative, widely adopted in news reporting and business analysis. Compared with general video understanding, data video understanding places greater emphasis on accurately reading data from animated charts, integrating evidence across charts and time, and understanding how narrative organization and visual design communicate information. Yet existing benchmarks target either general videos or static charts, and data video understanding has not been systematically evaluated. We present DataVista, the first benchmark for data video understanding, containing 961 real-world data videos and 6,775 evaluation questions organized under a three-level progressive capability framework (data perception, temporal reasoning, narrative understanding) with 10 fine-grained question types across five topic domains. Systematic evaluation of 19 mainstream MLLMs shows that the best-performing model, Gemini-3.1-Pro, achieves 70.0% overall accuracy, still far below human expert performance, with models performing worst on Causal Reasoning and Narrative Structure. Increasing frame counts and adding subtitles mainly benefit data perception and temporal reasoning, with limited gains in narrative understanding. Further analysis of model responses identifies typical failure modes in chart reading, evidence judgment, and instruction understanding. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DataVista.

</details>


### [104] [Memory Forcing: Attendable Mid-Horizon History for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11756)
*Jiaming Zhang,Xinyu Wang,Huafeng Shi,Gangshan Wu,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Memory Forcing 提出了一种优化的视频流媒体方法，通过重新分区缓存以保留中间事件和近期运动，克服了中程遗忘的问题，并且该设计能够扩展到更大的模型，生成更现实和动态的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频流媒体方法存在中程遗忘的问题，导致视频质量下降。Memory Forcing 旨在解决这一问题，通过改进缓存管理来继续利用视频中的过去事件，从而提升流媒体质量。

Method: Memory Forcing 设计了 Archive & Working Banks 以区分存储区、归档区和工作区，并引入了 Bank-aware RoPE 技术重新定位绝对时间索引，确保每个分区在注意力时点上保持独特性。

Result: 在 1.3B 量级的模型上，Memory Forcing 在较长的视频片段上表现最佳，且从 5s 到 60s 长度之间的性能下降最小。而扩展到 Wan2.2 5B 模型后，该方法生成的视频更具有物理合理性、真实感和动态性，这是该领域首个公开的 5B 模型。

Conclusion: Memory Forcing 在缓存管理和时间对齐方面展现了创新，成功解决了中程遗忘问题，并且适用于不同的模型量级，提供了更高质量的视频流媒体体验。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion enables causal video streaming without a bidirectional pass over the full clip, but existing few-step systems usually retain only the opening and most recent frames in a fixed-size KV cache. Once an event leaves this window, later frames can no longer attend to it, a failure we term mid-horizon forgetting. We present Memory Forcing, a few-step streaming method that preserves this missing history without increasing the cache size. Its Archive \& Working Banks partition the cache into sink, archive, and working regions, retaining diverse intermediate events alongside recent motion under fixed memory. Because absolute temporal indices drift outside the training range, Bank-aware RoPE reassigns indices at attention time so each bank remains distinguishable. At 1.3B, Memory Forcing leads on longer clips, shows the smallest drop from 5s to 60s among methods reporting all four lengths, and preserves subjects and scenes through leave-and-return. The same design scales to Wan2.2 5B, producing more physically plausible, realistic, and dynamic videos and, to our knowledge, the first public 5B model on this forcing line.

</details>


### [105] [From Video Clips to Creation Trajectory: Sora100K for AI-Native Video Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11770)
*Sicong Yang,Ruihuan Yang,Jian Lu,Jianfei Yuan,Xiaodong Cun,Xiuli Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sora100K数据集，它将AI原生视频创作流程视为结构化的视频创作轨迹，通过视频创作轨迹和分解的三个部分及其注释，改进了视觉质量、多镜头生成和跨镜头一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频生成和编辑数据集将这些任务视为分离阶段，而本文旨在构建一个连贯的视频创作工作流程，以适应AI原生的视频创作方法。

Method: 通过识别视频创作轨迹，并将它们分解为生成记录、编辑边的单轮次和多轮次编辑轨迹，进而通过VLM和严格构造管道进行注释和重建，最后使用LTX-2模型评估监督价值。

Result: 实验结果显示，Sora100K在视觉质量、多镜头生成和跨镜头一致性方面有所改进，但多轮编辑指令的连续跟随仍然是挑战。

Conclusion: Sora100K为AI原生视频创作提供了一个新的数据基础，超越了孤立的视频片段，朝向结构化的视频创作轨迹。

Abstract: AI-Native video creation is shifting from isolated video clips toward iterative video creation workflows. However, existing datasets remain largely video clips, representing video generation and editing as separate tasks rather than connected stages of a video creation workflow. In this paper, we introduce Sora100K, a dataset that represents the AI-Native video creation workflow as a structured video creation trajectory. Specifically, we first identify video creation trajectories and decompose them into three subsets according to their structural roles: text-to-video generation records as roots, single-turn video editing records as editing edges, and multi-turn video editing records as complete trajectories. Then, we use a VLM to assign semantic annotations for generation roots and editing-operation annotations for editing edges. A strict construction pipeline further reconstructs source-to-edit lineage, editing order, and intermediate video states while ensuring data quality. Finally, we perform lightweight adaptation on LTX-2 models to assess the supervision value of Sora100K. The results show improvements in visual quality, multi-shot generation, and cross-shot consistency, while successive-turn evaluation reveals that following multi-turn editing instructions remains challenging. Sora100K establishes a new data foundation for AI-Native video creation beyond isolated video clips and toward structured video creation trajectory. The dataset and supplementary materials are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ysicong/Sora100K.

</details>


### [106] [Skill-V: Verifiable Self-Evolving Skill Library for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11781)
*Jie Ma,Zhipeng Qian,Yufei Ma,Zihan Liang,Jiayi Ji,Qingpeng Cai,Ben Chen,Peng Jiang,Xiaoshuai Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Skill-V 是一种验证式的自我进化的技能库，通过版本化的可证伪契约来表示技能，利用环境结果来驱动库的进化，确保技能的有效性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我进化的技能库主要通过积累新知识来提高，但忽视了测试和修正已有知识的重要性。Skill-V 的引入旨在提供一种可靠的知识进化方式。

Method: Skill-V 通过版本化的可证伪契约将语义意图与可观察的行为标准联系起来，利用任务失败来驱动技能添加，任务结果来引导现有技能边界的修订，并通过验证修订以保护语义约束和不退化以前的证据结果。

Result: Skill-V 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上分别实现了 95.3% 和 85.9% 的成功率，还保持了比增长导向基线更紧凑的技能库。适用性意识过滤器减少了错误技能调用，结果导向的修订修正了错误指定的技能边界，而不会影响以前观察到的证据的性能。

Conclusion: 可靠的知识进化不仅仅是积累经验，还需要学会保留哪些知识，何时进行修正，以及何时进行应用。

Abstract: Interactive agents can turn experience into reusable skills, yet existing self-evolving skill libraries primarily improve by accumulating new knowledge. Failures may lead to new skills, while previously stored skills are less often revisited as new evidence arrives. However, growth alone does not ensure reliability, as a retrieved skill may be inapplicable under the current task conditions, and an existing skill may encode a mis-specified operational boundary. Reliable skill evolution therefore requires not only adding knowledge, but also testing and revising what is already stored. We introduce Skill-V, a verifiable self-evolving skill library. To make stored knowledge testable, we propose representing skills as versioned, falsifiable contracts that link semantic intent to observable behavioral criteria. We use environment outcomes to drive library evolution. Specifically, task failures motivate skill addition, while disagreements between contract evaluations and task outcomes guide revisions to existing skill boundaries. To validate these revisions, we require them to preserve protected semantic constraints and satisfy non-regression criteria for rubric-outcome metrics on historical replay evidence. Finally, we employ an applicability-aware filter to exclude candidates judged confidently inapplicable to the current task. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, Skill-V achieves success rates of 95.3% and 85.9%, respectively, while maintaining a more compact skill library than growth-oriented baselines. Applicability-aware filtering reduces incorrect skill invocations, and outcome-grounded revisions correct mis-specified skill boundaries without degrading performance on previously observed evidence. These results show that reliable skill evolution requires more than accumulating experience: the library must learn which knowledge to retain, when to revise it, and when it should be applied.

</details>


### [107] [Phase-aware video generation for physics-grounded dynamics and interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11791)
*Jingfeng Ou,Kun Wang,Rui Zhao,Jingwei Guan,Limin Wang,Chao Dong,Xingyu Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAVG通过双重分支架构和时空交叉注意力来生成固体-气体动力学的物理合理视频，改善了运动一致性、物理合理性和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体和气体具有不同的动力学特性且彼此相互作用，使得生成物理上合理的固体-气体动力学视频具有挑战性。因此，PAVG旨在通过明确建模固体和气体的动力学，捕捉它们之间的物理相互作用，来解决这一问题。

Method: PAVG采用双重分支架构来分别建模固体和气体的动力学，并使用时空交叉注意力机制来捕捉它们之间的物理相互作用。它还构建了一个包含超过70万物理轨迹的仿真库，以促进该任务。

Result: PAVG生成的视频在运动一致性和物理合理性方面优于现有方法，同时在视觉质量方面也有所提升。

Conclusion: PAVG提供了一种创新的方法来生成固体-气体动力学的物理合理视频，这为相关领域的研究提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Generating physically plausible videos for solid-gas dynamics is challenging as different phases exhibit distinct dynamics yet remain coupled through physical interactions.
