*Zijun Wang,Zewen Liu,Minhua Lin,Zhaotian Weng,Zhan Shi,Bing He,Yisi Sang,Dakuo Wang,Benoit Dumoulin,Wei Jin,Yuyin Zhou,Cihang Xie,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用语言模型作为研究世界模型（RWMs），预测候选干预措施在不同研究环境中的效果。通过实验数据，RWMs能够显著提高未见干预措施的预测准确性，并且研究知识不仅限于环境内使用，也可以跨环境重复利用，从而在限定的实验预算下提升自我改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于AI研究代理在提出实验和在真实环境中执行实验之间的能力不平衡，研究提出了探索语言模型是否可以作为研究世界模型，通过预测候选干预措施的效果来促进研究环境下的递归自我改善。

Method: 通过对2600多个实验记录的评估，涵盖预训练、后训练和推理三个研究环境，建立研究世界模型来预测未见干预措施的效果。使用不同领域的研究经验和知识，评估模型的泛化能力。

Result: 研究发现，具有内环和跨环研究知识的研究世界模型（RWMs），相较于无经验的设置，能够大幅降低选择后悔（78%），并分别增加15.8%和11.6%的最佳收益。同时，在没有进一步模型优化或增加推理努力的情况下，添加研究知识就能够显著提高干预措施的排名。

Conclusion: 研究表明语言模型可以作为有效的研究世界模型，通过积累实验数据，该模型展示了提升研究自我改善的潜力。未来的研究应致力于收集更多领域的实验数据以训练更准确的研究世界模型。

Abstract: AI research agents automate the cycle of proposing, implementing, and evaluating experiments, opening a path toward recursive self-improvement. Yet their ability to propose experiments outpaces their capacity to execute them in real environments, making outcome prediction a key capability for sustained self-improvement under limited experimental budgets. We investigate language models as Research World Models (RWMs), which predict the outcomes of candidate interventions across research environments. Our evaluation draws on over 2,600 experimental records from nine research environments spanning pretraining, post-training, and inference, representing more than 171,000 H100 GPU-hours of experimentation. Research knowledge acquired from real experimental experience improves RWM predictions of unseen interventions within the same environment (Spearman +0.27), and can be reused across environments. For example, using only pretraining experience from OLMo3, Marin, and Nanochat, an RWM reduces selection regret in the Qwen3 environment by 78% compared with zero-experience setting. These benefits extend to multi-round Autoresearch under a fixed selection budget: RWMs with in-env and cross-env research knowledge increase the best gain achieved by 15.8% and 11.6%, respectively. Ablations across 13 language models used as RWMs show that adding research knowledge can improve intervention ranking more than changing models or increasing reasoning effort alone. These findings support language models as RWMs and motivate accumulating experimental data for future RWM training.

</details>


### [251] [TokenRouter: Efficient Serving System for Token-Level LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12242)
*Tianyu Fu,Tengxuan Liu,Ruoxi Wang,Yixin Dong,Yi Ge,Yichen You,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokenRouter 提供了一种高效、易于开发者使用的 LLM 推断服务系统，专门处理基于标记级别的路由推理，显著提高了解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单一 LLM 假设导致了在标记级别路由推理时严重的步进脱同步和频繁的批次准入延迟，并给开发人员带来了较高的实现复杂度。针对这些挑战，设计了 TokenRouter，一种基于请求的编程、模型为中心的执行的高效且开发者友好的 LLM 推断服务系统。

Method: TokenRouter 采用基于请求的编程，模型为中心的执行方式。开发者从单一请求的角度描述路由逻辑，运行时为每个 LLM 启动一个子服务器，并异步调度请求。每个子服务器使用延迟批处理调度器，其最优超参数来自系统的数学吞吐量模型。

Result: TokenRouter 在不同路由算法、负载和模型对上的解码吞吐量比现有系统提高了 2.01 到 64.15 倍，显著提升了标记级别 LLM 路由的服务效率。

Conclusion: TokenRouter 成功解决了标记级别路由的效率挑战，提供了适用于 LLM 推断的高效、友好系统，并开源了其代码，为未来研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing distributes inference work across different models, advancing the cost-quality Pareto frontier of LLM serving. While coarse-grained routing at the session or query level has been widely adopted in production systems, recent algorithmic work shows that fine-grained token-level routing can yield substantial efficiency and quality gains. However, efficiently serving token-level routed inference poses significant challenges to existing systems. Built on single-LLM assumptions, current systems suffer from severe step desynchronization and frequent batch admission delays under token-level routing, and they also impose high implementation complexity on developers. To address these challenges, we design TokenRouter, an efficient and developer-friendly serving system for token-level routed LLM inference. TokenRouter follows the principle of request-centric programming, model-centric execution: developers describe routing logic from the perspective of a single request, while the runtime launches a subserver for each LLM and dispatches requests asynchronously. Each subserver employs a delayed-batching scheduler, whose optimal hyperparameters are derived from a mathematical throughput model of the system. Across diverse routing algorithms, workloads, and model pairs, TokenRouter achieves 2.01-64.15x higher decoding throughput than existing systems, substantially advancing the serving efficiency of token-level LLM routing. Our code is available at https://github.com/thu-nics/TokenRouter.

</details>


### [252] [EgoVoice: Proactive Spoken Assistance from Egocentric Multimodal Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12248)
*Heeseung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EgoVoice框架通过利用Egocentric场景下的人类操作视频，训练并评估了一个新的主动式说话助理系统，显著提高了干预时机和内容的相关性，以及用户的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在连续的第一人称流中决定何时发言和说什么的问题上表现不足，因此开发了EgoVoice框架，旨在通过人类操作视频提高主动式说话助理的表现。

Method: 从HoloAssist视频中分离干净的音频流，并将每段视频转换为必须在每个时刻决定是否提供语音指导的格式。使用多模态预训练语言模型并优化其干预行为。

Result: 实验表明，现有系统在时机和内容相关性上的表现较差，而EgoVoice在干预时机、内容相关性以及用户偏好方面都取得了显著的改进。

Conclusion: EgoVoice框架提升了主动式说话助理的性能，为未来的发展提供了潜在的道路。

Abstract: Wearable augmented reality (AR) assistants are moving toward continuous real-world interaction, where they perceive the user's activity through first-person video and audio and provide timely spoken guidance without being explicitly asked. While proactive video assistants, spoken dialog systems, and egocentric task understanding have each advanced rapidly, existing systems do not address the joint problem of deciding when to speak and what to say from continuous first-person streams. We introduce EgoVoice, a framework for training and evaluating proactive egocentric spoken assistants. From HoloAssist video recordings of real human instructors, we construct clean audio streams through source separation and speech resynthesis, and convert each video session into a format where the model must decide at each moment whether to remain silent or provide spoken guidance. We fine-tune an omni-modal LLM with our data, and further improve its proactive intervention behavior with direct preference optimization. Experiments across closed and open-source models show that existing systems rarely produce well-timed, meaningful proactive interventions, while EgoVoice yields clear improvements in intervention timing, content relevance, and human preference over the zero-shot backbone.

</details>


### [253] [HarnessSQL: Harness-Native Training for SQL Agents in Realistic Database Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12274)
*Haolin Yang,Jipeng Zhang,Jian Xie,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HarnessSQL 是一种在训练后框架，通过保留完整的交互结构，结合强化学习和监督微调，显著提高小模型在 Text-to-SQL 任务上的执行精度，并有效应用于交互基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的 Text-to-SQL 模型无法反映真实场景下的数据库交互，导致训练和部署间的差距。HarnessSQL 旨在解决这一问题，通过模拟实际交互环境，优化模型性能。

Method: HarnessSQL 通过创建孤立的、可执行的数据库环境，将隐式执行或acles 隐藏起来，并在目标 SQL 框架内部直接部署教师，仅保留验证过的轨迹用于序列监督微调，随后进行执行奖励强化学习。

Result: 在 Spider 2.0-SQLite 上，HarnessSQL 显著提高了紧凑模型的执行准确性，如将 Qwen3-8B 的执行精度从 15.5% 提高到 45.2%，Qwen3-14B 从 22.2% 提高到 54.8%，同时在 BIRD-Interact 和 LiveSQLBench 等交互基准测试中表现出有效迁移。

Conclusion: 训练数据库代理直接在其执行框架内是掌握复杂、长期数据库工作流程的关键。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL models are commonly trained to map questions directly to static queries, whereas real-world database agents operate through stateful, multi-turn interaction with live databases -- inspecting schemas, executing probe queries, diagnosing errors, and revising hypotheses. This creates a critical train-deploy mismatch, as the execution harness that mediates this interaction is introduced only at inference time. To bridge this gap, we propose HarnessSQL, a harness-native post-training framework that preserves the full interaction structure throughout both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. HarnessSQL builds isolated, executable database environments paired with hidden execution oracles, rolls out teachers directly inside the target SQL harness, and retains only verified trajectories for full-sequence SFT, followed by execution-reward RL. Across Spider 2.0-SQLite, HarnessSQL dramatically boosts the execution accuracy of compact models, raising Qwen3-8B from 15.5% to 45.2% and Qwen3-14B from 22.2% to 54.8%, while transferring effectively to out-of-distribution interactive benchmarks such as BIRD-Interact and LiveSQLBench. Our findings demonstrate that training database agents directly within their execution harness is essential for mastering complex, long-horizon database workflows.

</details>


### [254] [VFold: Symmetry-Aware Cross-Layer Value Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12338)
*Neha Verma,Sungwon Kim,Kenton Murray,Kevin Duh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种不对称值缓存合并策略，能够在不损害性能和不需更改架构的情况下减少缓存内存占用，同时还能与现有的缓存压缩技术组合使用，实现更高的压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的缓存压缩技术要么需要对LLM架构进行改变，要么压缩效果不佳，研究旨在找到一种新的方法，能够在减少内存占用的同时，避免对性能产生负面影响。

Method: 提出了一种称为对称感知的值缓存合并策略，通过分析层间缓存的相似性来进行有效的压缩。

Result: 该策略能够在保持性能的同时，减少缓存内存占用，并且可以与其他缓存压缩技术结合，实现更高的压缩比。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了值缓存中未充分利用的能力，提供了一种简单而有效的方向，可以在内存受限的情况下扩大上下文窗口。

Abstract: While caching key-value (KV) states accelerates Large Language Model (LLM) decoding, this cache can dominate memory usage at long context lengths. One solution is to compress this memory by exploiting inter-layer cache similarities. However, most existing techniques necessitate architectural changes to LLMs and incur substantial overhead. In this work, we propose a symmetry-aware value cache merging strategy that reduces cache memory while avoiding both harmful performance degradation and architectural overhead during decoding. Furthermore, we show that this approach can be exploited alongside existing cache compression techniques, composing with high-ratio quantization or key cache pruning to reach compression ratios that neither method reaches alone, with minimal additional cost. Ultimately, our findings reveal a major source of underutilized capacity in the value cache, offering a simple yet highly effective direction for scaling context windows under memory constraints.

</details>


### [255] [Which Skill to Distill? SGUID: Selecting a Compact Skill Bank for Model-Skill Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12367)
*Yuhan Liu,Xiyao Ma,Zhongkai Sun,Xu Han,Chengyuan Ma,Benjamin Z. Yao,Chenlei Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种名为SGUID的方法，用于在模型蒸馏过程中选择有效的技能集合，从而提高LLM下游任务的表现。实验结果显示，经过多次筛选和蒸馏，少量精选技能可以达到或超过使用全部技能的效果，且SGUID有助于模型和技能的稳定共进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法主要通过语义相关性从技能库中检索技能，而忽视了每个技能的实际有用性。作者认为，通过有效选择和蒸馏技能，可以更好地优化模型的性能。

Method: 作者首先在政策导向蒸馏中观察到不足25%的技能提供了有用的蒸馏信号，随后提出了SGUID方法，该方法保留的技能通过持续有效的学习信号来筛选。SGUID还支持根据模型更新后的策略轨迹构建新的候选技能库，并选择要内化的技能。通过两轮筛选，精简后的技能集合能够显著提升模型性能。

Result: 研究表明，经过两轮选择和蒸馏，仅从6个精选技能中进行蒸馏，就能够达到或超过使用完全技能库的效果。在包含两个不同系列的四个模型中，SGUID都展示了稳定提高模型性能的能力。特别是在第二轮筛选中，实现了对Qwen3-8B模型的显著提升，HMMT25得分提高了1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 总的来说，SGUID为LLM下游任务的稳定模型-技能共进化提供了有效的方法。这种方法不仅能够减少技能库带来的负担，还能够保证模型在连续蒸馏过程中的性能提升。

Abstract: Skills, reusable procedural guidance added at inference, can substantially improve LLM downstream performance (Li et al., 2026). Prior work retrieves skills from a bank by semantic relevance, then uses them as inference-time patches or for model distillation. The individual utility of each skill, however, is largely neglected. We first show that, in on-policy distillation where skill-conditioned policies serve as teachers, fewer than 25% of retrieved skills provide useful distillation signals. We then propose SGUID, a method for selecting a compact subset of skills for distillation. SGUID retains a skill only if it consistently yields effective learning signals during training. The selected skills are then distilled to produce a better model. Our results show that not all skills are worth distilling. Across four models from the Olmo and Qwen families, distilling 6 selected skills matches or exceeds full-bank distillation in mean avg@12 on three of the four models, and on all four after a second round that distills 3 newly selected skills, while the full banks are up to 11x larger. Importantly, SGUID supports stable model-skill co-evolution: after a distillation round, a new candidate bank is curated from the updated model's rollouts, and SGUID selects which skills to internalize next. In the second round, this loop selects 3 new skills and improves Qwen3-8B from 64.3% to 66.3%. The selection step is essential for stability: on Qwen3-4B, naively updating the model with unfiltered skills degrades performance, including a 0.3 percentage point drop on HMMT25, whereas SGUID improves HMMT25 by 0.5 points after the first round and 1.1 points after the second. These results identify skill selection as the key mechanism for stable model-skill co-evolution.

</details>


### [256] [Latent Core Tokenizer: Compress, but Meaningfully](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12376)
*Felermino D. M. A. Ali,Millicent Ochieng,Ogbemi Ekwejunor-Etchie,Ade Famoti,Jacki O'Neill,Debjit Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LCT 提出了一种新的分词方法，它在 token 压缩的同时考虑了语言结构和频率，从而在多个基准测试中表现优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词方法在紧凑词汇表的情况下，无法均匀地分配语言能力，因此需要一种新的分词方法来解决这个问题。LCT 通过分离结构发现和词汇构建，利用最小描述长度、熵和形态学约束来识别可重用的语义单元，并构建共享的词汇表。

Method: LCT 使用最小描述长度原则和熵为基础的边界信号来识别可重用的语义单元，同时也考虑了形态学规则。这些单元被用来构建一个共享的词汇表，而不是只关注于压缩效率。

Result: LCT 在104种语言上实现了比 BPE、Unigram 和 PBBPE 更低的生育率和更高的 MorphScore，同时保持了可比性的跨语言分词成本差异。在四个多语言下游基准测试中，LCT 的综合得分分别提高了 1.48、1.83 和 2.00 个点。

Conclusion: LCT 的研究表明，仅仅是压缩并不能预测表示质量，而形态学驱动的结构发现和频率的合理分配对于提高表示质量至关重要。

Abstract: Tokenizers are commonly optimized for compression, but a compact vocabulary does not necessarily distribute its capacity evenly across languages. We introduce the Latent Core Tokenizer (LCT), a language-agnostic approach that separates structural discovery from vocabulary construction. LCT uses Minimum Description Length, entropy-based boundary signals, and morphotactic constraints to identify reusable linguistic units before constructing a shared vocabulary. Across 104 languages with a 200K-token vocabulary, LCT achieves lower fertility and higher MorphScore than BPE, Unigram, and parity-aware BPE, while maintaining comparable cross-lingual disparity in tokenization cost. Across four multilingual downstream benchmarks, LCT improves aggregate score by 1.48, 1.83, and 2.00 points over BPE, Unigram, and parity-aware BPE, respectively. Our findings show that compression alone does not predict representation quality and highlight the importance of morphology-driven structural discovery and how frequency is used to allocate the final vocabulary across languages.

</details>


### [257] [Predicting Alignment Generalization with Value Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12410)
*Andy Liu,Mehar Bhatia,Karolina Stanczak,Mona Diab,Vered Shwartz,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析LLM模型在不同价值观下的泛化能力，发现基于模型激活的表示方法比基于文本描述的方法在预测泛化效果上更有效。研究还表明预测泛化能力有助于评估模型的鲁棒性，并提出了LLM价值观的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改善语言模型的行为与人类道德价值观的契合度，研究者们在模型微调后需要了解模型在新情境下的表现。虽然模型在狭窄行为上的训练取得了高匹配度，但在新场景中的行为仍可能产生意料之外的结果。该研究通过预测模型在新的价值观下的行为变化，解决了这一问题。

Method: 研究者采用了大规模数据分析的方法，评估了66种现代对齐目标中包含的值域。他们在预测泛化任务中测试了基于模型激活和基于文本描述两种代表性的技术，并发现基于模型激活的方法表现更佳。

Result: 研究发现，基于激活的方法在泛化效果上表现出明显优势，其相关度达到0.45，而基于描述的方法仅为0.05。通过对泛化能力的应用，研究者还发现，多值对齐目标中价值之间的相似性显著影响模型的鲁棒性。研究进一步提出了LLM价值观的分类，基于实际的泛化动态。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了在LLM模型中研究价值泛化的重要性和其在实际应用中的指导意义，提出的方法为模型行为的更实际和科学的设计与训练提供了新框架。

Abstract: LLM developers post-train their models to exhibit prosocial values and behavioral traits, which are enumerated in an alignment target. However, while recent post-training developments have yielded models that score highly on alignment evaluations, training models on sets of narrow behaviors still influences their behavior across unseen contexts and environments in unexpected ways. In this paper, we establish the task of alignment generalization prediction, i.e., predicting how fine-tuning a model to follow a given value changes its behavior across a wide range of held-out values. We conduct a large-scale analysis of alignment generalization effects across 66 values found in modern alignment targets, and benchmark representational techniques on the alignment generalization prediction task. We find that representations based on model activations when applying values in context significantly outperform methods based on textual descriptions of the values. Specifically, the best activations-based methods achieve correlations of 0.45 with our generalization matrix, compared with 0.05 from description-based baselines. We then show the applicability of representations that predict alignment generalization toward downstream tasks by using them to measure how similar the values in a multi-value alignment target are, which we find is significantly correlated with model robustness. Finally, we show initial evidence towards a shared, model-independent value space, which we use to develop the first taxonomy of LLM values grounded in empirical generalization dynamics. Our work demonstrates the importance of studying value generalization in LLMs and its application toward the more empirical design and training of model behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [A Modular Event-Driven Software Architecture for Open-Source Industrial IoT Edge Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10544)
*Pei Yu Wong,Thien Tran,Hudyjaya Siswoyo Jo,Jonathan Kua*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种模块化的事件驱动软件架构，用于开源IIoT边缘网关。该架构通过容器化的网络堆栈，实现了Modbus到MQTT的协议转换，并在边缘层设计了一个本地事件引擎，以降低网络延迟。初步部署和验证结果显示，该架构具有良好的集成时间缩短和高运行稳定性，为实现实时基于边缘的工业自动化提供了坚实的软件基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业环境中的云基础设施整合老式的工业机械设备面临巨大的软件工程挑战。由于边缘控制器大多具有刚性和专有性，这导致了耗时的手动配置过程。论文的目标是通过提供一个模块化的事件驱动软件架构，来解决这些挑战，以减少系统集成时间并提高运行稳定性。

Method: 本文方法包括构建容器化网络堆栈来实现Modbus到MQTT的协议转换，同时在边缘层设计了一个本地事件引擎，直接处理条件逻辑并显著减少网络延迟。最后，验证了该架构在工业级Raspberry Pi Compute Module 4 (CM4)上的鲁棒性、可扩展性和用户友好性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该提出的架构在系统集成时间上实现了显著的减少，并保持了高运行稳定性。这强有力地展示了为实现实时基于边缘的工业自动化提供了可靠的软件基础。

Conclusion: 论文通过介绍了一个模块化的事件驱动软件架构，为工业物联网的边缘集成提供了新的解决方案。该架构不仅减少了集成时间，还证明了其在工业环境中的适应性和稳定性。

Abstract: Integrating legacy industrial machinery into modern cloud infrastructures poses a significant software engineering challenge. Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) deployments frequently rely on rigid and proprietary edge controllers that require extensive manual configuration. Open-source single-board computers (SBCs) offer a highly adaptable hardware alternative, but they still lack standardized software deployment frameworks capable of bridging localized serial protocols with global cloud environments. In this paper, we present a modular event-driven software architecture designed specifically for open-source IIoT edge gateways. We built a containerized web stack to provide a unified architecture that abstracts the complexities of Modbus-to-MQTT protocol translation, enabling rapid configuration of telemetry streams to cloud platforms such as Amazon Web Services (AWS) IoT. Furthermore, we designed a localized event engine that processes conditional logic directly at the edge layer, which significantly reducing network latency. The proposed architecture is preliminarily deployed and validated on an industrial-grade Raspberry Pi Compute Module 4 (CM4) to investigate its robustness, scalability and user-friendliness. Experimental results demonstrate a substantial reduction in system integration time and high operational stability, thus establishing a robust software foundation for real-time edge-based industrial automation. This paper contributes to extending the lifespan of legacy machinery by enabling seamless integration with modern industrial infrastructure.

</details>


### [259] [CacheFit: The Rising Cost of Cache Replacement and Constraint-Driven Redesign of Last-Level Cache Replacement Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10579)
*Bita Aslrousta,Kaushal Mhapsekar,Darsh Asher,Azam Ghanbari,Joshua Kalyanapu,Samira Mirbagher Ajorpaz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了CacheFit，这是一种新的替换策略设计循环，它通过将电路级成本纳入搜索目标中，从一开始就考虑替换策略的成本。使用CacheFit设计的策略在保留类似或更好性能的同时，可以显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文认为，成本作为优化目标参与设计过程会改善替换策略的设计。在实际中，替换策略的成本通常是在实现阶段后期才关注的，这使得成本无法影响设计的早期阶段。论文旨在改变这种做法。

Method: CacheFit方法结合了ChampSim模拟器、HARCOM电路级成本模型和一个大型语言模型。该方法在编写算法时对每个候选策略进行评分，考虑它们在延迟、面积、功率和加速方面的表现。设计师可以设定面积、功率和延迟预算，并保存每次提案和学习到的内容。

Result: CacheFit被应用于两个现有策略MPPPB和SHiP++。首先对MPPPB进行优化，得到Fit-MPPPB，面积减少了60.3%，静态功率减少了56.3%，晶体管数量减少了58.9%，虽然IPC提高了1.1%，但在SPEC CPU2017测试中的命中率提高了7.5个百分点。对SHiP++进行了优化，得到的Fit-SHiP++在面积减少了56%，静态功率减少了61%，晶体管数量减少了56%，虽然IPC降低了1.54%，但在SPEC CPU2017测试中的命中率降低了2.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结论是，从成本角度设计替换策略，可以显著降低成本，同时保持或改进性能。这种方法能够引导设计师更早地考虑成本问题，从而提高整个设计流程的效率和成本效益。

Abstract: The latency, power, and area costs of LLC replacement remain poorly explored not because simulators cannot be instrumented--event counters feeding a power model are routine--but because no cost model takes a replacement policy as input. CACTI and McPAT price regular structures, a cache or a core, not the small, oddly shaped arrays and dependent logic a policy is made of, and no event count reveals a victim-selection path's logic depth. Without a model, cost has never been an objective a policy search could optimize.
  In practice, a policy is first selected in simulation for its IPC, and its cost is reduced later in RTL, after the algorithm is fixed. Cost thus enters the design too late to shape it. This paper asks what happens when cost is present from the start.
  We build CacheFit, the first policy-design loop for LLC replacement with circuit-level cost inside the search objective. CacheFit connects ChampSim to HARCOM, a circuit-level cost model, and to a large language model that proposes policy variants. Every candidate is scored on latency, area, power, and speedup while its algorithm is still being written. The designer sets the area, power, and latency budgets, and every proposal and every lesson the search learns is stored as readable text that the designer can check and edit.
  We apply CacheFit to two published policies. From MPPPB, the most expensive baseline in power and transistors, it produces Fit-MPPPB: 60.3% less storage, 56.3% less static power, and 58.9% fewer transistors, with 1.1% higher IPC and a hit rate 7.5 percentage points higher on SPEC CPU2017. From SHiP++, the highest-IPC baseline, it produces Fit-SHiP++: 56% less storage, 61% less static power, and 56% fewer transistors, for 1.54% lower IPC and a hit rate 2.1 points lower. A policy designed with its cost in view can therefore be far cheaper to build at similar or better performance.

</details>


### [260] [A Survey on LLM-Integrated Hardware Design Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10580)
*Hao Zheng,Jaime Rafael Imperial,Bardia Nadimi,Xiangfei Kong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本综述文章系统地回顾了大型语言模型在硬件功能验证中的应用，涉及SystemVerilog断言生成、激励生成、错误定位及设计修复、模型检查与等价性检查、SAT/SMT优化以及新兴自主验证工作流等几个领域。文章指出LLM在验证中的有效用法，并强调了语义对齐、工具集成、泛化能力和综合评估等关键挑战。文章还探讨了神经-符号自主验证系统等新兴方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文章旨在为研究者和从业者提供关于大型语言模型在硬件验证领域应用的全面理解，指明发展方向和面临的挑战。

Method: 文章通过系统性回顾文献，按照方法论、验证目标、工具交互、基准测试和评估标准组织材料。文章还分析了推理时技术和训练时适应。

Result: 文章总结并指出了LLM在硬件验证中的有效应用案例，但同时也指出了语义对齐、工具集成等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 文章强调了需要构建能够结合LLM灵活性和可验证证据的自主验证系统，从而提高验证的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being integrated into hardware verification to automate specification interpretation, verification-artifact generation, debugging, formal reasoning, and tool orchestration. This survey provides a systematic review of LLM-assisted hardware functional verification across SystemVerilog assertion generation, stimulus and testbench generation, bug localization and design repair, model checking and equivalence checking, SAT/SMT optimization, and emerging agentic verification workflows. We organize the literature by methodology, verification objective, tool interaction, benchmark, and evaluation criterion, and examine both inference-time techniques--including prompting, retrieval, structured reasoning, and agentic workflows--and training-time adaptation. Across these areas, a common pattern emerges: LLMs are most effective as semantic reasoning, search, and orchestration components embedded within verification-aware workflows, while simulators, formal engines, coverage tools, and solvers provide executable feedback and correctness evidence. However, tool acceptance alone does not establish verification correctness, since assertions, tests, repairs, or proofs may satisfy available checks without faithfully capturing the complete design intent. We therefore identify semantic alignment between specifications and verification evidence, scalable integration with deterministic tools, generalization to unseen designs, and rigorous evaluation of correctness, cost, robustness, and human effort as key challenges. Finally, we discuss emerging directions toward specification-centered, neuro-symbolic, and persistent agentic verification systems that combine LLM flexibility with independently checkable verification evidence.

</details>


### [261] [RFChipAgent: Multi-Agentic AI Flow for Analog/RF Chip Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10858)
*Awani Khodkumbhe,Yunfei Feng,Raj Rangarajan,Kevin Wang,Kamal Sahota*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: RFChipAgent 是一种多智能体系统，旨在通过大型语言模型代理人实现端到端的射频电路设计自动化，通过多模态信息检索增益生成（RAG）子系统、拓扑智能体、电路图和测试平台智能体，闭环混合电路尺寸校准引擎以及信任分数评分仿真数据库四个技术支柱来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频（RF）电路是数字计算与物理世界之间的关键接口，随着如Wi-Fi 7到6G等新兴标准的要求不断提高，而目前的RF设计依然是芯片开发中最劳动密集的步骤。

Method: RFChipAgent 采用了四个技术支柱来实现RF电路设计的自动化：多模态检索增强生成子系统、拓扑智能体、电路图和测试平台智能体、闭环混合电路尺寸校准引擎以及信任分数评分仿真数据库。

Result: RFChipAgent 在GF22FDSoI 60 GHz广泛频带的毫米波低噪声放大器（LNA）拓扑上的实验验证表明，设计自动化可以显著减少设计工作量，同时保持签收级验证质量。

Conclusion: 这项工作奠定了基于LLM的多智能体电子设计自动化（EDA）的基石，特别是针对模拟/RF电路。

Abstract: Analog/RF circuits remain the critical interface between digital computation and the physical world, and emerging standards from Wi-Fi 7 to 6G place stringent demands on them, yet analog/RF design remains one of the most labor-intensive steps in chip development. We present RFChipAgent, a first-of-its-kind multi-agent flow of large language model (LLM) agents for end-to-end analog/RF circuit design automation, in which AI agents collaboratively orchestrate the complete design flow under human supervision. RFChipAgent is built around four technical pillars. First, a multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) subsystem with private per-document FAISS indexing extracts design knowledge from existing engineering documentation. Second, a topology agent drives topology selection, and a schematic and testbench agent automates circuit and testbench assembly. Third, a closed-loop hybrid circuit-sizing engine combines Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) and CMA-ES optimization, evaluating every candidate in a simulator-in-the-loop framework. Fourth, a trust-scored simulation database accumulates verified performance data and builds an adaptive optimization model that informs subsequent trials. We validate RFChipAgent on a family of GF22FDSOI 60 GHz wideband mm-wave low-noise amplifier (LNA) topologies, demonstrating automated topology generation, specification-driven design-space exploration, and simulator-guided optimization. Experimental results show substantial reductions in design effort while maintaining signoff-quality verification. This work establishes a foundation for LLM-driven multi-agent electronic design automation (EDA) for analog/RF circuits.

</details>


### [262] [The Missing Fourth Term for the Emulation Tensor Memory Equilibrium (TME) Model: The Residue Deconstruction Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10924)
*Harun Bayraktar,John Gunnels,Peter Caday*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究修正了TME模型中关于Ozaki方案II仿真fp64执行时间计算的遗漏项，补充了输入解构成本，并据此计算出内存瓶颈操作下性能的理论上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者修正了原始论文中的计算模型，以提供更准确的执行时间估算，并强调了特定算子的实际性能界限。

Method: 作者通过修正TME模型，加入输入解构成本的计算，并使用cuBLAS仿真路径进行验证，从而推导出新的操作强度阈值。

Result: 修正后的模型显示，对于内存瓶颈操作，仿真的性能最多只能达到原性能的0.3-0.9倍，对于7点stencil仅能达到1.8倍，且指出了特定算子需达到的具体指令数才能突破限制。

Conclusion: 修正后的模型为理解特定硬件上特定算子的实际性能提供了理论依据，同时指出了仿真的性能极限。

Abstract: The Tensor-Memory Equilibrium (TME) model of "FP8 is All You Need (Part 1)" calculates the execution time of Ozaki Scheme II emulation of fp64 as the maximum of a tensor-core term and a High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) traffic term, plus a per-output reconstruction term. However, it omits the per-input deconstruction cost: every streamed fp64 operand must be scaled, rounded, and reduced modulo each of the $r$ moduli on SIMT pipes before any matrix multiply can issue. In this note we add this fourth term, calibrate its constant from the cuBLAS emulation path, and derive a closed-form operational-intensity threshold $\mathrm{OI}^{*} = c_q r P_{\mathrm{fp64}}/(8P_{\mathrm{int}})$ below which emulation cannot match native fp64 regardless of tensor-core throughput. On the NVIDIA B300 GPU the threshold is $\mathrm{OI}^{*}\approx 0.56$ FLOP/B. As a result, GEMV, SpMV, and low-batch GEMV, which are the memory-bound kernels the original paper claims to accelerate, are limited to 0.3-0.9x of native performance, and the 7-point stencil to 1.8x rather than the claimed 3.1x. Dense GEMM is unaffected as expected. We also show that precomputing and storing the residues moves the same cost into the bandwidth term, and we state the instruction count that an implementation would have to achieve to invalidate the bound.

</details>


### [263] [ATLAS: Adaptive TDA-guided Landscape-Aware Transistor Sizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10985)
*Youngmin Oh,Jihwan Won,Yuntae Park,Bosun Hwang,Suwan Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）的贝叶斯优化（BO）框架ATLAS，用于模拟电路设计自动化。它能够在没有观测到可行区域的情况下，通过构建映射图来估计连接区域，从而更有效地探索设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化方法如贝叶斯优化（BO）和强化学习（RL）在模拟电路设计中由于忽视了设计空间的拓扑结构，可能导致优化过程局限于非最优的设计区域。研究旨在利用TDA提高设计效率。

Method: 该方法采用了一个贝叶斯优化框架ATLAS，通过在每次迭代中构建映射图来估计连接区域，随后对候选点进行分类并注入目标探索奖励，最终减少对设计可行性的模拟次数。

Result: 在GF180和SKY130工艺的四种模拟电路基准测试中，ATLAS方法的表现优于包括RL和BO在内的基线方法，能够显著减少模拟次数。

Conclusion: 这是首次将TDA应用到模拟电路设计自动化中。此工作为模拟电路设计自动化提供了一种新的有效方法。

Abstract: Analog transistor sizing, finding design parameters that simultaneously satisfy multiple performance specifications, is a labor-intensive bottleneck in circuit design. To support analog circuit experts, various automation methods have been proposed, including Bayesian optimization (BO), reinforcement learning (RL), and others. Yet existing methods are oblivious to the topological structure of a feasible design space, which can fragment into disconnected regions due to operating-regime transitions, conflicting specification trade-offs, and nonconvex device physics. This topological blindness causes the optimizer to converge within a single feasible region while missing others that may contain superior designs. To address this limitation, we propose ATLAS. a BO framework utilizing Topological Data Analysis (TDA). At each iteration, a Mapper graph is constructed over a surrogate-predicted feasible region to estimate connected regions, enabling topology-aware exploration from the very first iteration without any observed feasible points. A topological sensitivity score classifies candidates as bridge, frontier, or interior points, injecting a targeted exploration bonus into the acquisition function. Experiments on four analog circuit benchmarks in the GF180 and SKY130 processes demonstrate that \coin finds feasible designs with significantly fewer simulations than baselines, including RL and BO methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply topological data analysis to analog circuit design automation. The
  official implementation is publicly available on https://github.com/youngmin0oh/atlas.

</details>


### [264] [DynaTE: Accelerating Diffusion LLMs via Dynamic Token Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11284)
*Minghan Jiang,Jiayi Wang,Shuaiting Li,Haibin Shen,Kejie Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: DynaTE是一种硬件与软件协同设计的架构，能够动态适应dLLM解码过程中不断变化的token状态，通过跳过低效的token计算、利用动态token依赖性、以及应对不规则的输出变化等方式，提高了加速器的运行效率和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的dLLM加速器在处理具有并行迭代特性的dLLM时，存在计算资源利用率低、能效不足的问题。DynaTE旨在通过动态适应token状态优化加速器执行，从而解决上述问题。

Method: DynaTE通过以下三个方面来实现其目标：1）允许自适应token执行，通过跳过低效token的计算，同时在不同活跃token模式下保持高利用率；2）通过FLDD在当前迭代中细化一小部分本地依赖的token，减少总体去噪迭代次数，并通过Merge--Split--Merge数据流隐藏由此产生的串行开销；3）引入流式词汇引擎，以适应选择性计算和不均匀词汇需求导致的不规则输出变化。

Result: 在两个代表性的dLLM上，DynaTE实现了2.05-2.78倍的速度提升和2.99-3.93倍的能效提升，相较于最先进的dLLM加速器，其速度提高了2.55倍，能效提高了6.07倍，相较于Jetson AGX Orin，则分别提高了6.07倍和3.84倍。

Conclusion: DynaTE通过优化dLLM加速器的执行，成功提高了系统的整体性能，并展示了其在实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion-based LLMs (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) LLMs by enabling bidirectional parallel refinement, alleviating the sequential decoding bottleneck of AR generation. However, their parallel iterative refinement mismatches AR accelerators optimized for sequential decoding and their discrete token generation differs from DiT accelerators designed for continuous denoising. Recent dLLM accelerators have explored workload-specific optimizations to reduce vocabulary processing overhead and redundant computation across denoising iterations. However, these approaches retain all tokens in parallel execution, despite varying token refinement utility and execution requirements.
  This paper presents DynaTE, a hardware--software co-design architecture that dynamically adapts accelerator execution to evolving token states during dLLM decoding. DynaTE first enables adaptive token execution by skipping low-utility token computation, while a dimension-reconfigurable PE array maintains high utilization under varying active-token patterns. Second, DynaTE exploits dynamic token dependencies through FLDD to refine a small number of locally dependent tokens within the current iteration, reducing the overall number of denoising iterations, while a Merge--Split--Merge dataflow hides the resulting serial overhead. Third, a streaming vocabulary engine interleaves multiple token streams from the LM head to accommodate irregular output variations caused by selective token computation and uneven vocabulary-selection demands.
  Evaluated on two representative dLLMs, DynaTE achieves 2.05--2.78$\times$ speedup and 2.99--3.93$\times$ higher energy efficiency over state-of-the-art dLLM accelerators, while delivering 2.55$\times$ speedup and 6.07$\times$ higher energy efficiency over Jetson AGX Orin.

</details>


### [265] [DEX: Digit-Level Early Exit for Energy-Efficient MSDF Neural Network Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11748)
*Yousef Sadegheih,Dorit Merhof,Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于MSDF算术的加速器，用于量化的U-Net分割，通过在推理过程中的几个机制减少计算量，同时保持较高的Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤分割中的U-Net推断需要大量计算，因此需要动态减少计算量的硬件，而不是依赖固定的精度或静态模型压缩。

Method: 该方法采用了MSDF算术，支持带符号的INT8运算，并在流中进行偏置累积。提出了四种运行时机制以减少计算量。

Result: 与浮点模型相比，所提机制在73个保留案例中实现了80.58%的平均Dice分数，减少了38.38%的数字周期。仅使用精确机制就减少了18.79%的周期。

Conclusion: 该加速器以500MHz运行，在0.858mm²的面积上消耗0.726mJ用于每个192x192的块。预计八输出加速器的延迟为16.6ms，每块功耗为1.67mJ。

Abstract: U-Net inference for brain-tumor segmentation requires billions of multiply-accumulate operations, motivating hardware that can reduce computation dynamically rather than relying only on fixed precision or static model compression. Most-significant-digit-first (MSDF) arithmetic exposes the leading digits of a result during computation, enabling output-dependent decisions before the full value is generated. This paper presents an MSDF accelerator for quantized U-Net segmentation with a two-stage grouped processing element supporting signed INT8 operands and in-stream bias accumulation. Four runtime mechanisms operate directly on the output digit stream: exact early negative detection (END) in ReLU layers, exact sign-only decision making in the segmentation head, calibrated low-order-digit skipping, and calibrated pruning. The two approximate mechanisms are selected offline under an accuracy constraint, while execution requires only lightweight control and does not modify the stored weights. On a residual U-Net trained with nnU-Net for BraTS, the proposed mechanisms reduce digit cycles by 38.38\% while achieving a mean Dice score of 80.58\% on 73 held-out cases, compared with 81.20\% for the floating-point model; the exact mechanisms alone reduce cycles by 18.79\% without altering the quantized output. Synthesized in 45~nm, the processing element operates at 500~MHz, occupies 0.858~mm$^2$, and consumes 0.726~mJ per $192\times192$ patch under switching-activity-annotated power analysis. A projected eight-output accelerator with shared activation delivery achieves 16.6~ms latency and 1.67~mJ per patch.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [An Explainable Header-Centric Framework for Large-Scale Semantic Table Interpretation and Data Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10541)
*Marcelo Valentim Silva,Hannes Herrmann,Valerie Maxville*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种解释性强、以列头为中心的框架，用于无需单元格值的情况下进行元数据的列类型标注和数据质量评估，并通过解析标签和验证规则识别数据质量问题，提出了HeadersIQ衡量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的知识图谱质量管理依赖于下游图验证和集成后的表数据质量，但本研究强调在集成前，通过元数据（特别是列头）确保数据的质量和可靠性的必要性，尤其是在单元格值不可用、噪声大或不合适的场景下。

Method: 该框架利用精心策划的词典资源将列头映射到39种可解释的FinalFormat类型，并且保持了标记来源的可追溯性。通过对不同类型的数据质量问题进行分类和聚合，该方法能生成HeadersIQ这种轻量级且无权值的数据源级别的质量度量。

Result: 该研究在多个异构基准测试中得到了积极的结果，成功地覆盖了噪声较大的真实世界元数据，并通过盲目的诊断审核对多种匹配问题进行了详细分析，证实了跨不同源和标准类型的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可重用的工作流，用于基于元数据的语义注释、数据源级别的质量监控以及知识图谱导向的基准诊断，旨在增强知识图谱构建过程中的数据质量控制。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph (KG) quality depends not only on downstream graph validation, but also on the quality of tabular metadata used before integration. In metadata-only Semantic Table Interpretation (STI), where cell values are unavailable, noisy, or unsuitable, column headers become a critical source of semantic evidence for traceable KG preparation.
  We present an explainable, header-centric framework for metadata-only Column Type Annotation (CTA) and Data Quality Assessment (DQA). The framework maps headers to 39 interpretable FinalFormat types using curated lexical resources and preserves token-level traceability through SourceKeywords. Each assigned type activates validation rules based on a taxonomy of Data Quality Issues (DQIs), producing detections such as missing data, duplicates, domain violations, wrong data type, and temporal mismatch. These detections are aggregated into HeadersIQ, a lightweight, unweighted data source-level quality metric.
  The framework was evaluated across heterogeneous benchmarks, including UCI, Prague, Kaggle, VizNet/Sato, SOTAB, T2Dv2, and the SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG track, comprising around 120,000 header columns. The results show broad practical coverage across noisy real-world metadata, while a parallel KG-mapping pathway supports alignment to DBpedia and Schema.org. On the SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG track, the official GT-strict evaluation was modest. However, a blinded diagnostic audit indicates that many mismatches reflect benchmark granularity, aliasing, and ontology-selection effects rather than wholly implausible header-centric predictions. We report this audit as diagnostic evidence on disagreement patterns, not as revised benchmark performance. Overall, the paper presents a reusable workflow for metadata-driven semantic annotation, data source-level quality monitoring, and KG-oriented benchmark diagnosis.

</details>


### [267] [Synthesis Through Simulation: Generating Coherent Enterprise Data via Scalable Agent-System Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10549)
*Yipeng Li,Ashutosh Hathidara,Jane Lo,Harshavardhan Abichandani,Gunraj Singh,Atin Ghosh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Synthesis Through Simulation (STS) 提出了一种无需数据库模式的模拟方法，该方法通过运行时执行操作生成数据，从而确保结构有效性并独立解决分布建模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为企业AI中的工具调用代理规模训练和评估受限，STSA提供了一种新的数据合成方法，以解决结构有效性和模式可用性之间的固有限制，以及基于过程的方法在分布拟合方面的不足。

Method: STS 使用语言模型代理在模拟的企业环境中通过政策约束的API执行操作来生成数据。这种方法确保了结构的有效性构建，并将有效性验证与分布建模解耦，从而使两者可以独立解决。

Result: Generalist Populator (GP) 在没有访问数据库模式的情况下实现了数据生成。GP 达到了所有环境中平均边际拟合度0.88，100% 的约束满足度。相比之下，统计合作者对七个项目不适用，而模式特权代理在航空环境中82%的轨迹上失败。

Conclusion: Synthesis Through Simulation 开源了完整的框架、十个模拟环境以及所生成的数据集，为解决大规模企业AI训练和评估提供了新的可能途径。

Abstract: Tool-calling agents have become central to enterprise AI, yet training and evaluating them at scale remains severely constrained due to business and legal restrictions on enterprise systems, data, and database schemas. Tabular data synthesis offers a natural alternative, but its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by structural validity and schema availability, while procedure-based approaches yield the opposite weakness, typically lacking distributional fidelity without per-domain authoring. We introduce **Synthesis Through Simulation** (STS), a **schema--free** data synthesis paradigm in which an LLM agent generates data by executing operations against policy-enforcing APIs within simulated enterprise environments. Because data is generated through the same environment that defines what is valid, STS guarantees structural validity by construction while decoupling validity enforcement from distribution modeling, allowing each to be addressed independently. The **Generalist Populator** (GP), STS's domain-agnostic agent, addresses the remaining challenges of distributional fidelity and synthesis scalability: GP achieves **0.88** average marginal fidelity and **100\% constraint satisfaction** across all ten environments *without access to DB schemas*, while statistical synthesizers are inapplicable to seven due to necessary seed data requirements, and schema-privileged agents fail 82\% of trajectories on airline environment's tightly coupled workflows due to brittle task composition. We open-source the full framework, all ten environments, and generated datasets at https://github.com/SAP/synthesis-through-simulation.

</details>


### [268] [Agent-Controlled Forgetting for Tool-Using Agents: Reversible Context Curation in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10590)
*Jan-Peter Franke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了代理控制遗忘的概念，通过让代理选择以前观察到的工具结果并替换为简短笔记，同时保留原始内容在可恢复的存档中，以减少资源消耗并降低API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工具使用代理的数量增加，其携带的观测内容也越来越繁杂，有用信息量远小于原始负载。因此，研究提出了一种代理控制遗忘的方法，旨在提高效率并优化资源利用。

Method: 研究采用Python框架，提供批量归档和显式恢复功能，同时保护用户指令和助手消息不受这些操作的影响。方法包括选择工具结果并替换为简短笔记，保留原始内容在可恢复存档。

Result: 在两种操作中，使用该方法比保留原有历史记录减少了50%的累计输入令牌，并节省了约3.3倍的API成本。然而，两者均未能完全通过后续评估，且方法在请求次数和时间上有所增加。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在嘈杂的工具使用轨迹中，这种方法可以在一定程度上节省资源，并且认为工作负载依赖性是可逆上下文管理的核心考虑因素。

Abstract: Tool-using agents repeatedly carry observations whose useful content can be much smaller than their original payload. We study agent-controlled forgetting: the acting model selects previously observed tool results, replaces each with a short note at its original position, and retains the exact original in a recoverable archive. A Python harness exposes batch archival and explicit recovery without task-specific model training, while protecting user instructions and assistant messages from these operations. In an exploratory OpenTelemetry debugging case followed by an unrelated implementation task, the method ended with 231,951 provider-reported prompt tokens versus 912,492 under retained history, used 50% fewer cumulative input tokens, and had an estimated API cost of USD 1.28-1.44 versus approximately USD 4.38. Both arms passed the two-case primary behavioral oracle; neither fully satisfied the follow-up evaluation. The method made more requests and took 17% longer. A contrasting application-development pair produced no context or cost saving, and an earlier continuation exhibited lower manually assessed quality despite reduced context. These observations demonstrate substantial resource savings in noisy tool-use trajectories and identify workload dependence as a central consideration for reversible context management.

</details>


### [269] [Verification and Self-Improvement in Agentic AI: Foundations and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10611)
*Chien-Ping Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过有界验证与隐藏终端随机性来比较提高代理型AI系统的机制，包括搜索更长时间、获取额外支持以及修改如何提出和验证输出。证明了独立多数放大保持语言，而随机接受可能会引入错误输出。框架将自我改进与验证资源正确性、可接受的语言以及选择错误联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在通过构建一个框架来比较提高代理型AI系统性能的三种不同机制，并探讨这些机制如何影响系统的自适应能力和验证过程。

Method: 本文采用有界验证的方法，定义了阶段结构，如允许传输的范围、多项式边界、交替验证协议和终端检查器，以及使用随机性和确定性验证的比较研究。

Result: 研究证明独立多数放大可以保持语言，而随机接受可能会导致错误输出。同时也探讨了随机验证者类别的复杂性边界问题，以及对递归自改进的支持情况。

Conclusion: 本文的结论是，通过精确验证（零随机性情况）可以满足正确性要求，而随机验证则需要更多的审计和保障措施来确保证据的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems can improve by searching longer, receiving additional support, or modifying how they propose and verify outputs. A performance score does not distinguish these mechanisms. We compare these changes through bounded verification with hidden terminal randomness. A stage specifies admissible transcripts, polynomial bounds, an alternating verification protocol, and a terminal checker. Its native reach uses default support; its closure frontier permits all support already admitted by the interface. Under a uniform pointwise probability gap and task-relative soundness, these are well-defined languages. We prove that independent majority amplification preserves both languages, whereas existential acceptance over random tapes can admit incorrect outputs. Exact verification is the zero-randomness case, with placement and completeness results. The randomized-verifier classes satisfy $Σ_k^{\mathrm{P}}\subseteqΣ_k^{\mathrm{RV}}\subseteqΣ_{k+1}^{\mathrm{P}}$; strict enlargement and depth separation require explicit complexity assumptions, while $\mathrm{BPP}=\mathrm{P}$ yields exact companions with the same frontiers. Representation analysis separates invariant acceptance from core-versus-support labels that can change under refactoring. For recursive self-improvement, uniformly bounded self-modification under a common sound interpreter and fixed verification protocol remains within the same verification class. A separate conditional-error budget controls false selection across adaptively chosen candidates. A quota-enforced XOR-synthesis family separates unbounded ratios of search success from changes in the accepted languages; exact and probabilistic audits check the resulting evidence requirements. The framework ties self-improvement claims to obligations on correctness, admissible evidence, verification resources, and selection error.

</details>


### [270] [The Harness as the Only Mutable Surface: Compliance-Bounded Self-Evolution of LLM Agents in Credit Pipelines, with a Measured Admission Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10629)
*Ravil Akhtyamov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种限制性自进化机制，通过固定模型权重并记录每次适应的变化，确保每次调整都有原因和测试记录。实验表明该机制有效减少了误报，并能准确识别有害变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发出一种能够安全且审查可追溯的自进化机制，以确保AI模型的可信度和合规性，特别是在信用评估等高风险领域。

Method: 通过构建一个受限制的双重循环引擎，该引擎在每次部署前记录一个哈希链式记录。此外，使用模拟的代理和种子搜索提案者而非语言模型来进行评估。

Result: 经过三种类型的监督重新解释测试，在不同风险级别和种子条件下，受限制的循环引擎仅接受了144个候选变化，均未增加误差，且避免了49%的潜在误报。而无限制系统则接受了309个有害变化，错误率进一步升高。

Conclusion: 固定模型权重并严格记录每次变化可以显著提高自进化系统的审查可追溯性，有助于确保高风险应用中的AI决策可靠性。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents can adapt a credit pipeline to a changed rule, but an agent that rewrites itself destroys the artefact a supervisor reviews: a named change, a recorded test, an approval. We argue that self-evolution is reviewable only if it is confined to the runtime harness (instruction text, tool-call logic and primitive composition) while model weights stay fixed, so that every adaptation is a diff with a cause and a test attached. We give a dual-loop engine built on that bound, with one admission gate that writes a hash-chained record before deployment, and we measure the gate in simulation, with a simulated agent and a seeded-search proposer rather than language models. Across three families of supervisory re-interpretation at three severities, 10 seeds each, the gated loop admitted 144 of 7,449 candidate changes, none of which worsened error on held-out history, and restored the false-positive rate to the oracle level without raising missed flags in every low- and mid-severity cell. With the gate replaced by the check an unbounded system applies (fewer errors visible in recent traces), the same loops admitted 309 harmful changes and left missed flags above 10% in 49 of 90 runs: false positives fell because the screen was loosened. Evaluated on pre-shift labels, the gate rejected every candidate, so a re-interpretation must be encoded as a rule that relabels history. Parametric and scope shifts were repaired locally, a structural one only by primitive replacement; at the highest structural severity the gate's fixed tolerance blocked the correct replacement in half the seeds. We map the mechanisms to the EU AI Act's provisions for high-risk credit scoring and note that the April 2026 US model-risk guidance excludes agentic AI from its scope.

</details>


### [271] [Speaking the Navigator's Language: Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translation for Frozen Aerial VLN Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10635)
*Xi Chen,Zhe Liu,Xiaogang Xu,Jiafei Xu,Chunyi Zhou,Yuan Su,Rui Zeng,Tianyu Du,Kelu Yao,Chao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过提出一种针对意图中心的Weak指令翻译器（TGIT），解决了飞行导航中的指令差距问题，成功提高了指令执行的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前飞行导航系统使用的指令与用户实际提供的简短意图驱动指令之间存在差距，导致成功率下降。为了提升性能，作者开发了一种翻译器，将原始详细指令转化为意图中心的Weak指令，进而提高导航成功率。

Method: 作者使用人类编写的样例提示语言模型，将原始指令转化为意图中心的Weak指令，并引入了一种前端Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translator (TGIT)，该系统利用导航轨迹结果来将Weak指令转换为可执行的导航命令。

Result: 通过对Weak指令的训练，翻译器能够将Weak指令的成功率提高至37.93%，并将针对冻结导航器的零样本转移到真实的人类指令（11.33%增至32.51%），同时提高了OpenFly测试的成功率（4.95%增至20.79%），并在CityNav和AirVLN数据集上进行了恢复。

Conclusion: 研究提出的方法能够有效提升飞行导航系统在实际用户指令下的成功率，且具有较强的泛化能力。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents are typically trained on detail-rich, trajectory-aligned commands, whereas users issue short, intent-driven instructions; on a frozen OpenFly navigator, this \emph{instruction gap} drops success rate (SR) from $31.03\%$ to $11.33\%$. To scale translator training, we prompt a language model with human-written style examples to convert original commands into paired, intent-centered Weak commands, which yield $15.27\%$ SR. We introduce the \textbf{Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translator (TGIT)}, a front-end that keeps the navigator frozen and translates Weak inputs into agent-executable commands by learning from its trajectory outcomes. The resulting Weak-trained translator raises Weak-input SR to $37.93\%$ and transfers zero-shot to real human instructions ($11.33\%{\rightarrow}32.51\%$); it also improves held-out OpenFly ($4.95\%{\rightarrow}20.79\%$) and yields recovery on CityNav and AirVLN.

</details>


### [272] [Plan-and-Patch: Diffusion Language Models for Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10786)
*Syamantak Kumar,Jiang Guo,Hassan Hamad,Hideo Kobayashi,Yi Xiang,Yezhou Yang,Yanjun Qi,Daniele Bonadiman,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种Plan-and-Patch框架，利用扩散语言模型(dLLM)生成结构化计划并修复受损害的部分，无需重新生成整个计划。实验结果显示，与自回归模型相比，扩散模型能够更快地生成计划并提高修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期代理的成功执行依赖于有序协调子目标、工具使用和中间结果的能力，但由于环境变化或工具行为超预期，现有的规划假设可能会被打破。因此，一种能够快速修复和调整规划方法对于长期代理至关重要。

Method: 一种结合扩散语言模型(dLLM)和计划修复的方法。首先，dLLM并行生成详细的计划；其次，修复阶段通过填充损坏部分来局部调整计划，而非重写整个计划。研究还比较了扩散模型和自回归模型在不同任务下的表现。

Result: 在自然计划任务中，扩散模型在不具体任务训练的情况下，修复成功率几乎是自回归模型的两倍。经过特定任务训练后，两者在计划生成的成功率方面相似，扩散模型计划生成的平均延迟减少了39-46%。

Conclusion: Plan-and-Patch框架为长时隙代理更快的计划生成和更有效的修复提供了一种方法，尤其是在经过适当训练的情况下。

Abstract: Planning is increasingly important for long-horizon agents, where successful execution requires coordinating subgoals, tool use, and intermediate outcomes over many steps. Yet assumptions made during planning may be invalidated by the environment, tools may return unexpected results, or actions may fail. Effective agents must therefore not only generate plans, but also revise them. Such revisions often affect only part of a plan, leaving the preceding and subsequent structure intact. Rather than regenerate the entire plan and risk unnecessary changes, repair can regenerate the affected region conditioned on the preserved prefix and suffix. We introduce Plan-and-Patch, a plan-and-act framework in which a diffusion language model (dLLM) generates a structured, program-like plan through parallel unmasking and repairs it by filling in selected regions while keeping the surrounding steps fixed. We compare DreamReasoner-8B and Qwen3-8B as diffusion and autoregressive (AR) planners. On Natural Plan without task-specific training, diffusion (53.7%) achieves nearly twice the plan repair success rate of AR (27.0%). After task-specific training on agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and TextCraft, the planners achieve similar observed success in plan generation, while diffusion reduces mean plan-generation latency by 39-46% relative to AR. Our results show that Plan-and-Patch provides a framework for faster plan generation and effective plan repair in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [273] [Whose Ground Truth? Embracing Ambiguity in Human-Centered AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10805)
*Jingyao Wu,Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Per Rådberg Nagbøl,Julie Gerlings,Giovanni Leoni,Georgiana Fryza,Lars Pilegaard Thomsen,Jakob Mainz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI系统应转向建模可能的人类判断空间，并区分有意义的模糊性与注释噪声，以促进更加以人为本的AI，更好地反映人类解释的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地与人类互动并对其作出决策，理解人类对这些决策的解释变得尤为重要。传统机器学习和AI系统假设存在单一的绝对真理，并通过聚合或视为噪音来处理人类注释的差异。但对于许多以人为本的任务，人类的解释是内在模糊的，同一个输入可能有多个合理且有效的解释。将这种模糊性简化为单一目标可能忽视了人类感知、判断和体验的多样性信息。

Method: 本文没有提出具体的方法论，而是从概念上呼吁改变AI系统的开发和应用方式，以更好地反映人类解释的多样性。

Result: 本文为AI系统的开发与应用提供了新的视角，强调建模可能的人类判断空间的重要性，并揭示了有意义的模糊性与注释噪声之间的区别。

Conclusion: 本文呼吁AI系统应遵循这种新的视角来代表、学习、评估、部署和治理，从而促进更加以人为本的AI。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly interact with people and make decisions about them, understanding human interpretations becomes an important part of developing human-centered AI. Conventional machine learning and AI systems are largely developed under the assumption that a single definitive ground truth exists, with variability in human annotations often resolved through aggregation or treated as noise. However, for many human-centered tasks, human interpretation is inherently ambiguous, and multiple interpretations of the same input may be simultaneously reasonable and valid. Reducing such ambiguity to a single target risks overlooking meaningful information about the diversity of human perception, judgment, and experience. In this position paper, we call for a shift towards modeling the interpretation space of plausible human judgments, while distinguishing meaningful ambiguity from annotation noise. We argue that this perspective should guide how AI systems are represented, learned, evaluated, deployed, and governed, supporting more human-centered AI that better reflects the diversity of human interpretation.

</details>


### [274] [On the Clock: Towards Punctual and Productive Time-Budgeted AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10833)
*Aaron Wang,Neelabh Madan,Vlad Sobal,Matthew Trager,Michael Kleinman,Elman Mansimov,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管通过增强学习和时间信息注入可以提高代理遵守时间和有效使用时间的能力，但在任务性能上仍存在问题，代理难以利用额外时间提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索小规模语言模型（LLM）如何在明确的时间预算限制下有效运作，关注代理对时间的意识及其优化表现的能力。

Method: 研究使用Qwen3.6-27B和Qwen3-4B分别参与多个计算密集型游戏和任务，评估代理在不同时间预算条件下的表现，尝试通过增强学习和时间信息注入改进代理。

Result: 代理在仅提示中给出预算的情况下难以将预算转化为时间管理策略，即使通过增强学习和时间信息注入，代理仍不能充分利用额外时间改善任务表现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了时间遵守和有效时间分配之间的差距，提示未来研究需要关注如何使代理更有效地利用时间来提升任务表现。

Abstract: We study whether small LLM agents can operate effectively under explicit wall-clock time budgets by both respecting the allocated runtime and using available time productively. We evaluate Qwen3.6-27B on five competitions from MLE-Bench Lite and Qwen3-4B on Zork I (Jericho), two agentic benchmarks where additional computational time can meaningfully improve performance. In the simplest setting, where the budget is stated only in the prompt, agents fail to translate the stated budget into controlled use of time. These failures arise from gaps in time awareness, since the harness provides no timing feedback, but also because they cannot reliably anticipate the duration of actions, and do not have a learned mapping from available time to an appropriate strategy. We investigate two complementary classes of interventions: harness-based mechanisms that expose timing information and enforce deadlines, and reinforcement learning with budget-aware rewards. Injecting timing information through the harness substantially improves budget adherence for Qwen3.6-27B without measurable loss in performance, while enforcement hooks tighten adherence further. RL with GRPO achieves near-perfect budget adherence on Zork I and generalizes to held-out budgets not seen during training, but does not improve task performance over the untrained harness on MLE-Bench. Once agents are made to respect the budget, they still fail to use additional time to improve task performance. RL-trained policies learn when to stop but often fill extra time with repeated actions, and GRPO training on multiple budgets tends to collapse toward the strategy learned for the shortest budget. Our results reveal a gap between time adherence and productive time allocation, which remains a central challenge for budget-conditioned agents.

</details>


### [275] [Self-Supervised Keyframe Discovery for Horizon-Invariant Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10857)
*Prabin Kumar Rath,Omkar Patil,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Keyframe Mnemonics的新方法，用于非马尔可夫环境的行为克隆，该方法通过学习随机采样的过往观察结果来发现关键信息，并以此引导关键帧的选择，以条件行为策略来建立行动分布，实现了长时间境条件下的成功和泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决行为克隆在非马尔可夫环境中的长期依赖问题，现有方法如递归模型和注意力机制存在训练不稳定和上下文长度限制的问题，因此提出了一种新的自监督方法Keyframe Mnemonics来改善这一问题。

Method: 通过从随机采样的以往观察中学习目标，并将其作为关键帧选择的奖励，从而发现关键的信息（mnemonics），然后利用这些关键帧来训练行为策略。关键帧仅存放在策略的工作记忆中，确保模型能够长时间保留上下文。

Result: 在合成的记忆域中，使用mnemonics的行为策略成功率达到100%，且在未训练的超长时间范围内不会降低性能表现。此外，在机器人操作基准测试中，该方法超过最强基线13.9%，并在真实机器人上达到了20倍更长的持续时间。

Conclusion: Keyframe Mnemonics方法可以在长时间记忆条件下有效进行行为克隆，通过关键信息的提取降低了模型对上下文的记忆需求，并且在多种基准测试中展现了优越的性能。

Abstract: Behavior cloning (BC) in non-Markovian environments is a challenging problem because policies have to reason over contextual information over long horizons. Existing policy architectures rely on recurrent or attention-based mechanisms to capture long-term dependencies. However, recurrent models suffer from hidden-state collapse and gradient instability under backpropagation through time, while attention-based models are fundamentally limited by context length. To address these issues, we propose Keyframe Mnemonics, a novel self-supervised method that $\textit{discovers}$ a set of information-critical observations ($\textit{mnemonics}$) by learning an objective from randomly sampled past observations and using it as a reward for keyframe selection. We then train a BC policy that conditions on the discovered keyframes to model the action distribution. Under certain task-structure assumptions, our formulation provides context retention guarantees over an infinite horizon, while maintaining a small set of decision-relevant keyframes in the policy's working memory. We evaluate our method on synthetic memory domains, where mnemonic-conditioned BC policies achieve $100$% success rates (SR) and generalize to horizons orders of magnitude beyond training without performance degradation. Additionally, we evaluate on memory-intensive robot manipulation benchmark, achieving a $13.9$% average absolute SR improvement over the strongest baseline across $23$ tasks and retaining $80$% SR at $20\times$ longer horizons on a real robot. Code and videos are available at https://keyframe-mnemonics.github.io.

</details>


### [276] [Reading the Room: Foundations, Design, and Challenges of Normative Competence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10906)
*Andrea Wynn,Harsh Satija,Seokhyun,Baek,Anqi Liu,Eric Nalisnick,Gillian K. Hadfield*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过多智能体社区辩论环境探索了LLM的规范能力，发现基线LLM无法学习社区规范，且对规范的具体风格和模型敏感，显示出缺乏泛化能力。进一步地，LLM在伴随非规范行为时表现出无选择性的归因失败，即使模仿不必要的行为也会受到惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统的自主性增加，使其与人类社会的规范系统保持一致成为关键挑战，因此研究规范能力对于改善AI的社会适应性至关重要。

Method: 采用一个多智能体社区辩论的设定，通过引入合成规范来研究规范能力，同时探究不同风格规范和模型对规范学习的影响。

Result: 研究结果显示基线LLM在缺乏预训练知识的情况下无法学习社区规范，且对规范形式和模型敏感，缺乏泛化能力。还发现，当存在非规范行为时，LLM会无选择地模仿这些行为，这种归因失败现象即使在惩罚模仿不必要的行为时也依然存在。

Conclusion: 研究表明当前的AI系统在行为模仿方面表现出色，但在辨别社会强制秩序方面能力有限，因此需要进一步提升AI系统的规范理解能力。

Abstract: Human communities are governed by normative systems: shared standards that produce \textit{norms} dictating acceptable behavior, enforced through community sanctioning. Aligning increasingly autonomous AI systems with these norms is a central alignment challenge, complicated by the fact that norms are vast in number, change quickly, and are often arbitrary (e.g., dress or language conventions). Thus, alignment requires \textit{normative competence}: the ability to discern from interaction alone what norms a community enforces without relying on static pretrained knowledge. We introduce a multi-agent community debate setting, where access to debate is governed by synthetic norms, to study normative competence in isolation from pretraining exposure. We show that baseline LLM agents fail to learn norms even when doing so would improve their accuracy. We then experiment with various \textit{normative modules} -- architectural components for norm inference -- finding that norm-following is highly sensitive to both the style of norm and the model powering the normative module, suggesting a lack of generalizability. Furthermore, when idiosyncratic, non-normative behaviors accompany the true norm, LLM agents exhibit an unselective attribution failure: they indiscriminately copy idiosyncratic noise alongside enforced rules, a pattern that persists even when imitating unnecessary behaviors is explicitly penalized. To the best of our knowledge, our work is the first to operationalize and evaluate normative competence in LLMs, demonstrating that current AI systems excel at behavioral mimicry but lack the capacity to discern socially enforced order.

</details>


### [277] [StoreBench: A Live-Commerce Environment for Evaluating and Training Autonomous Operator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10942)
*Daksh Raghuvanshi,Ved Vedere,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StoreBench 是一种动态的在线服装商店环境，旨在评估大型语言模型的长期规划和经济判断能力，通过模拟真实世界中的不确定性和挑战，提供了比静态基准更深入的评估。七个前沿 LLM 在 11 种 30 至 45 天的场景和一年的模拟中表现不一，而人类专家表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为改进大型语言模型（LLM）的后训练能力，研究者提出 StoreBench，通过将 LLM 应用于一个动态的在线店铺环境，对其进行长期规划和经济决策能力的评估，以弥补静态环境中的不足。

Method: StoreBench 环境设计中采用了一种生产级的电商平台后端，构建了一个现实世界环境，模拟了24小时全天候的客户下单、供应商定价变化和市场突变等不确定性。通过使用这些相同的 29 个店铺操作工具，并设定一个时间窗口来避免模型延迟影响模拟时间。评价任务上使用七种最先进的 LLM 进行了长达一年的动态模拟。

Result: 在 StoreBench 环境中，No LLM 能够达到预定的智能任务处理策略，在许多场景中表现落后于人类专家，仅 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 达到了49%的成功率。一些模型在完整的模拟年份下显示出 dramatic 的性能提升，在 Qwen3.5-27B 在仅进行五次不同任务训练后，表现从 0.136 提升到 0.373。

Conclusion: StoreBench 环境为 LLM 提供了更为全面和真实的评估方法，有助于发现和改进 LLM 在真实世界商业环境中应用中的不足。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning environments are now a primary lever for improving large language model (LLM) capabilities in post-training, yet most agentic benchmarks remain static: the world moves only when the agent acts, the reward is a terminal verdict, and the pass bar is set arbitrarily. We introduce StoreBench, a live-commerce environment in which an agent runs a mid-size online apparel store on a production-grade commerce backend, testing long-horizon planning and economic judgment under uncertainty. Customers order around the clock, suppliers reprice and fail, and market shocks arrive with partial or no warning. The agent acts through the same 29 merchant tools a human operator would use, under a windowed operation budget that makes simulated time a function of actions taken, so model latency cannot influence simulated time. Pass thresholds are calibrated against scripted anchor policies, the reward is hardened against a catalogue of reward hacks, and every episode replays identically given a sequence of actions. We evaluate seven frontier LLMs on 11 scenarios of 30 to 45 days and a full simulated year, over three world seeds at matched reasoning effort. No model matches the scripted smart-triage policy on average: the best, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, passes 49% of task-seed cells against the heuristic's 97%. Human experts working through the same tools and budgets outscore every model (mean composite 0.708 vs. 0.700). Over a full simulated year under the Claude Code harness, most models show dramatic performance improvement. In a GRPO post-training run, Qwen3.5-27B trained on only five disjoint tasks raises its mean composite on the held-out evaluation tasks from 0.136 to 0.373. We release five example training-split tasks, ten sample trajectories, and the scoring and verification tooling; the full environment and evaluation suite are withheld to keep the benchmark uncontaminated.

</details>


### [278] [Learning How to Search for Plans with Exponentially Less Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10954)
*Dominik Drexler,Simon Ståhlberg,Markus Fritzsche,Blai Bonet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过学习搜索控制策略来寻找计划的方法，该策略被表述为一个带寄存器和模式的通用策略，同时定义了结构化终止的概念，确保每条执行的长度是对象数量的多项式级。通过深度优先搜索在多项式空间内找到计划，并在大量问题上优于现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的启发式搜索可能存储过多状态，本文通过学习搜索控制策略来解决这个问题，从而提高搜索效率并减少资源消耗。

Method: 本文的方法包括定义一个带有寄存器和模式的通用策略，引入‘choose’规则来加载对象和标记回溯点；定义结构化终止来控制执行长度；并使用深度优先搜索在多项式空间内找到计划。

Result: 通过学习得到的策略，该方法能解决1,709个测试任务中的1,890个，优于LAMA、BFWS和Levitron，并且大多数任务可以在一秒钟内完成。

Conclusion: 本文提出的搜索控制策略为解决状态空间过大的问题提供了一个有效的方法，证明了这种方法在实际应用中的高效性和普遍适用性。

Abstract: Heuristic search for a plan can store exponentially many states, even when its heuristic is almost perfect. We instead learn search control, one specification per domain, written as an indexical policy: a generalized policy with registers that hold objects and modes that sequence its rules. We add the choose rule, which loads an object into a register and marks a backtracking point, where one candidate suffices; every other rule must work for all of its outcomes and needs no search. Our main result is that structural termination, which rules out infinite executions, also bounds every execution by a polynomial in the number of objects. A depth-first procedure then finds a plan in polynomial space, however large the state space, with no list of visited states. The cost is time, exponential only in the choice depth, the number of real choices along an execution. Any class that such a policy solves therefore lies in NP, and in P at constant choice depth. We learn these policies with a language model in a counterexample-guided loop that certifies termination, verifies the training tasks, and keeps the choice depth small. With the learned policies, the procedure solves 1,709 of 1,890 test tasks of the IPC 2023 Learning Track and the Autoscale Agile suite, more than LAMA, BFWS, and Levitron, and most of them within one second and 100 MiB.

</details>


### [279] [How Narrative Wrapping Affects LLM Refusal: A Cross-Language Benchmark and Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11005)
*Zhankai Ye,Yanning Wang,Yukai Jin,Bo Mei,Fangyi Li,Wei Wang,Shangqian Gao,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了语言模型在不同语言和文体下对有害请求的响应，发现即使通过角色扮演或叙事包裹，模型仍可能响应有害请求。提出了GUISE基准和AXIS方法来增加模型的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨语言模型在面对有害请求时的安全性问题，尤其是模型如何拒绝这些请求，特别是在使用角色扮演或叙事包裹的情况下。

Method: 通过构建GUISE基准来系统的评估语言模型对有害请求的响应；通过AXIS方法，结合偏好优化和旋转目标来调整有害请求的表示，同时加入承诺目标以训练模型完全拒绝有害请求。

Result: 研究发现，即使在古典语言中，模型也存在85%以上的攻击成功率。使用AXIS方法后，模型的综合安全性和易用性得分最高。

Conclusion: 提出了AXIS方法，可以显著提高模型对有害请求的拒答能力，同时保持较高的易用性。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models often refuse a harmful request stated directly but answer the same request inside a role-play or narrative wrapper. We measure this vulnerability across languages and registers: attack success on Qwen3-1.7B is already 89.4% in English and 93.0% in modern Chinese, and reaches 95.7% in Classical Chinese. We build GUISE, a benchmark for systematically studying this vulnerability. It includes parallel requests in English, modern Chinese, and Classical Chinese, matched harmful and benign pairs, wrapper types held out for evaluation, and a stricter criterion that counts warn-then-answer responses as attack successes. Representation analysis shows that language and register move harmful-request representations only slightly away from the model's refusal direction, whereas narrative wrappers move them much farther away. We propose AXIS, which combines preference optimisation with a rotation objective that aligns harmful-request representations with the refusal direction and a commitment objective that trains the model to refuse completely rather than produce a warn-then-answer response. Across Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B and GLM-4-9B, AXIS achieves the highest combined safety and usability score among the compared methods.

</details>


### [280] [Curating Always-Loaded Context for LLM Agents: A Capacitated Assortment Model with Censored Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11007)
*Zexuan Liu,Yuning Yang,Tiancheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种由有限容量注意力来管理上下文文件大小的方法，提出了上下文清理问题的上限，探讨了如何从过往会话中学习并决定添加或删除指令，同时指出删除被忽视指令可能错误地去除了有益指令，并设定了在限制编辑审查数量的情况下，人类审查者的后悔度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的目标是解决大规模语言模型（LLM）会话中由于上下文文件过大会导致性能下降的问题。为了解决这个挑战，作者将其形式化为一种有容量限制的精选问题，分析了添加和去除指令的具体策略及其影响。同时，还探讨了如何利用过往会话数据来优化未来的会话。

Method: 作者提出了一个上下文清理框架，首先将上下文文件大小问题描述为一个有容量限制的精选问题，并证明了最优文件大小的上限。紧接着，作者分析了添加和去除指令的影响，证明了盲目添加所有具有独立价值的指令可能反而降低整体价值，同时也提出了在人为审查数量有限时的后悔度上限。最后，通过实证实验验证了不相关规则的影响。

Result: 研究结果包括最优文件大小的上限和添加、去除指令的原则，以及在特定条件下人类审查者应如何做出决策。实验结果进一步表明，从真实上下文文件中抽取无关规则会对语言模型的合规性产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 本文为解决大规模语言模型会话中由上下文文件导致的问题提供了一种管理和优化上下文文件的方法，并提出了该问题的优化策略和理论上限。

Abstract: At the start of every session, LLM agents load a fixed context file, such as $\texttt{AGENTS.md}$. Each loaded token in the file is charged again in every later round of the session, and these files can degrade performance as they grow in size. However, in practice, human or automated curators usually grow these files by appending.
  We formulate context curation as a capacitated assortment problem. Instructions consume tokens under a finite attention capacity; adding an instruction never raises the compliance of the others, while retained instructions incur a per-session setup cost.
  We prove an upper bound on the optimal file size, regardless of the number of available candidate instructions, and that appending every instruction with positive standalone value can be arbitrarily worse in net value than selecting an optimal subset. A token budget also limits the loss when the token price is underestimated.
  We then examine what can be learned from past sessions and how this information can guide decisions to add or remove instructions. Feedback is inherently censored: the benefits and harms of loaded instructions are observable, whereas missing instructions generate feedback only when their absence causes harm.
  In this setting, we show that deleting instructions ignored by agents can inevitably remove helpful ones. We characterize how much evidence should be collected before adding an instruction. Besides, we bound regret when human reviewers can inspect only a limited number of edits per period. Empirical experiments further show that irrelevant rules drawn from real context files reduce language-model compliance.

</details>


### [281] [Distillation for Incrimination and Distillation for Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11012)
*Sebastian Prasanna,Jacqueline Tay,Alek Westover*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种不同的蒸馏方法，DFI目标是转移模型的恶意行为，但不转移其隐瞒能力；DFC则相反，旨在转移模型的能力而不转移恶意行为。实验发现，DFI能够使学生更可能承认其隐藏的行为，而DFC能在不转移恶意行为的同时提高模型的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于强大的未对齐AI模型可能识别到对齐评估并进行策略性行为，使得直接审计变得不那么有效。本文为了解决这个问题，提出了两种蒸馏方法，DFI和DFC。

Method: 作者首先提出了两种不同的蒸馏方法：DFI (Distillation for Incrimination)和DFC (Distillation for Capabilities)。DFI目标是转移模型的恶意行为，但不转移其隐瞒能力；DFC则相反，旨在转移模型的能力而不是其恶意行为。接着，作者通过实验验证了这两种方法的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，在将审计平台上的秘密保护模型蒸馏到其指令微调模型时，DFI产生了更愿意承认其隐藏行为的学生模型，表明关于行为的知识比隐藏行为的能力更易转移。另一方面，DFC在不转移恶意行为的情况下，也能提高模型的能力。两种方法都有效减少了无意识的行为转移。这表明蒸馏可以被用来进行AI安全研究：用来揭露恶意模型的行为或提取其能力，而不转移其恶意行为。

Conclusion: 这两种蒸馏方法为AI安全提供了新的研究方向：既可以用于揭露恶意模型的行为，也可以用于提取模型的能力而不会转移其恶意行为。

Abstract: Powerful misaligned AI models might recognize alignment evaluations and strategically behave well on them, rendering direct audits uninformative. However, distilling such a model into a weaker benign student places the teacher in a Distillation Double Bind: if misalignment transfers, the student may conceal it less effectively, revealing evidence about the teacher; if it does not, the student may learn useful capabilities while remaining benign. We introduce two distinct distillation approaches, one targeting each outcome. Distillation for Incrimination (DFI) aims to transfer misalignment but not the ability to conceal it. Distilling AuditBench's secret-keeping models into their underlying instruction-tuned model produces students that are significantly more likely than their teachers to admit their hidden behavior when asked, suggesting that knowledge of the behavior transferred more readily than the propensity to conceal it. Confession gains largely disappear when the student does not share the teacher's pretrained base, so DFI should target the teacher's own pre-RL checkpoint, which is weaker than the teacher but shares its base model. Distillation for Capabilities (DFC) aims to transfer capabilities but not misalignment. Among several techniques we evaluate, two are effective: inoculation prompting and training for more epochs on fewer unique examples. Both preserve the capability gains of standard distillation while substantially reducing the subliminal transfer of an animal preference, our proxy for misalignment. Together, these findings demonstrate two ways distillation can be used for AI safety: incriminating misaligned models, and extracting their capabilities without their misalignment.

</details>


### [282] [AgentHorizon: Evaluating Agentic Judges for Long-Horizon Computer-Use Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11050)
*Xing Han Lù,Dheeraj Vattikonda,Sina Hajimiri,Fatemeh Pesaran Zadeh,Parishad BehnamGhader,Ghazwa Darwiche,Amirhossein Kazemnejad,Christopher Pal,Alexandre Drouin,Siva Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项名为AgentHorizon的基准测试，包含1,373个计算机使用任务（指令-轨迹对），旨在评估自动裁判对长期交互历史中隐藏证据的定位和验证能力。通过比较不同模型的表现，作者发现某些模型在使用工具时表现更好，而开放权重模型的表现则较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动裁判在长任务和跨应用程序方面缺乏可靠性，因此需要改进以提升其准确性和有效性。

Method: 作者设计了一个包含1,373个任务的基准测试，其中每个任务都有一对指令和轨迹。通过记录指令紧密相关的轨迹，并构建负对任务，作者能够评估裁判在区分成功和失败任务方面的能力。此外，作者还测试了多个裁判模型，并在不同环境下评估它们的表现。

Result: 最佳自动裁判GPT-5.5在基准测试的一部分中达到80.9%的平衡准确率。研究还发现，某些模型在使用工具时表现较好，而开放权重模型的表现较差。不同裁判在接收有效轨迹和拒绝失败轨迹方面的能力差异很大。

Conclusion: 这项研究强调了需要具备在长期交互历史中定位和验证隐藏证据的自动裁判的重要性。此外，研究为开发更有效的裁判模型提供了宝贵的基准测试数据和见解。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are capable of completing complex tasks, increasing the use of automatic judges to determine success, either for training or for evaluation without human involvement. Despite their flexibility, their reliability on long tasks spanning multiple applications remains unclear. A trajectory, composed of long sequences of screenshots and actions, may appear complete, but in reality violates constraints from the instruction or introduces an unwanted side effect. To identify these errors, a judge needs to examine the trajectory with respect to the user's instruction. To this end, we introduce AgentHorizon, a benchmark of 1,373 computer-use tasks (instruction-trajectory pairs) drawn from 166 hours of human-recorded trajectories spanning three operating systems. By recording trajectories for closely related instructions, we can construct negative tasks by swapping the instructions. This paired design evaluates judges on their ability to distinguish a successful trajectory from one that completed a similar (but incompatible) request. We release the benchmark under three splits: a frontier split, AgentHorizon (AH), a simplified split, AgentHorizon-Simple (AH-S), and a development split, AgentHorizon-Development (AH-D). We further evaluate eleven judges by (1) directly passing the full trajectory (with up to 300 screenshots and actions), and (2) using them as coding agents across five agent harnesses. We find that our best agentic judge, GPT-5.5, achieves 80.9% balanced accuracy on the AH subset. We find that tool-use improves certain models but results in worse performance for open-weight models, and that judges differ drastically in their ability to accept a valid trajectory and reject failed ones. Our findings highlight the need for judges that are capable of locating and verifying often hidden evidence that a task was properly completed inside long interaction histories.

</details>


### [283] [Beyond Imitation: A Framework and Benchmark for LLM-Assisted Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11087)
*Rachel S. Y. Teo,Yutaro Yamada,Shashank Kotyan,Yuki Imajuku,Tarin Clanuwat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种旨在提高自动审稿系统性能的验证中心化方法，通过构建一个可扩展的基准来评估系统识别逻辑矛盾的能力，并提出了一种多层次审稿框架，强调对手稿的详细理解优先于审稿生成，从而提高了对人工审稿实践的契合度，表现出高度的人类审稿分数一致性、高效的错误检测性能和互补的审稿重点视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于科学出版的快速增长对同行评审造成了压力，特别是对机器学习领域，导致审稿质量下降和审稿人工作负担增加。现有的对大型语言模型（LLMs）作为自动化审稿助手的评估多集中在模仿人类写作的评论上，而忽略了支持同行评审核心功能的需求。因此，本文提出了一个验证中心化视角来评估LLM辅助的同行评审，并通过提出多层次审稿框架来提高系统的性能和安全性。

Method: 该研究通过构建一个可扩展的基准来评估审稿系统的性能，该基准通过在会议论文中插入合成错误来评估系统识别逻辑矛盾的能力。同时，提出了一个多层审稿框架，强调在生成审稿之前对论文的详细理解。

Result: 研究结果表明，此方法与人类审稿评分高度一致，具有高水平的错误检测性能，并提供了审稿人集中精力的补充视角。此外，方法还验证了对对抗性操纵的持续漏洞，表明自动审稿系统需要更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文的研究结论强调了严格、以错误为中心的评估对于指导LLM基于工具的负责任部署的重要性，特别是在同行评审和其他关键的科学工作流程中。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific publishing has strained peer review, particularly in machine learning, raising concerns about declining review quality and increasing reviewer workload. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as automated review assistants, yet their evaluation has focused largely on imitating human-written reviews rather than supporting the core functions of peer review. Here, we introduce a verification-centric perspective on LLM-assisted peer review, emphasizing error detection as a critical and resource-intensive task. We present a scalable benchmark that evaluates review systems' ability to identify logical contradictions, constructed through synthetic insertion of errors into conference papers, yielding unambiguous evaluation targets and enabling systematic comparison. We further propose a Multi-Layered Review (MLR) framework that prioritizes detailed manuscript comprehension before review generation, aligning more closely with human reviewing practices while improving token efficiency. Across evaluations, our approach demonstrates strong alignment with human review scores, achieves high error detection performance, and provides complementary perspectives on reviewer focus. These improvements can be attributed to both the choice of the underlying LLM and the design of our system. At the same time, we corroborate persistent vulnerabilities to adversarial manipulation, underscoring the need for robustness in automated review systems.
